## 2. What the audits found and what changed

### The three HIGH findings that mattered most
1. **The Laggard's transfer was farmable.** The transfer rule ("nearest Loud witness") could be staged by a competent faction. Patched: engineered/staged transfers now dissipate the tether ("the Undertow doesn't transact"), and the no-witness case is defined. The protagonist's threat model is now load-bearing rather than asserted.
2. **The masquerade had no arithmetic.** Sweeper headcount, event volumes, containment costs, and the funding gap were all asserted, never quantified. Patched: a classified Compact table now carries the numbers (consistent with the "Seep frequency tripled since 2000" canon).
3. **The constitution contradicted itself.** Three files gave three different treaty histories (1949 vs. 1977 vs. 1979); faction origin dates contradicted across four files; a T6 stayed "continuously contained" through a decade the bible says half the Soviet vault network went unguarded. Patched: one reconciled chronology (charter 1949 → build-out 1963–68 → mandatory 1977 → implementing Second Protocols 1979), an "Origins and Predecessors" reference table, and Tunguska's lost-decade dormancy (the "continuous containment" claim is now an in-world institutional lie).

### Structural strengths confirmed by the audits
- The **anti-loophole finance section** (09 §9.5) closed conjured-wealth, precog-trading, and Tide-energy exploits before the auditors arrived; the auditors called it the template for closing new holes.
- The **five anti-billionaire mechanisms** (08 8.7) were judged "near-airtight" — independent, precisely scoped, with the Pale Court as the worked exception.
- The **26.4 six-reason leak analysis** and the **27.3 detection stack** were judged genuinely well-built.
- All five **Layer-5 mysteries** were confirmed to be genuine unknowns with resolution conditions — none was a contradiction wearing a mask.
- The protagonist's power design survived honest attack: at full enemy knowledge, **the bible gives Arthur leverage, not safety** — and canon supports a "Arthur dies here" branch without contradiction. No plot armor was found or added.

## 3. Simulations (Phase 5) — headline results
- **A (T4 in Manhattan), B (rogue Choir), C (lost Russian T5), D (Vesper device), H (open faction war), I (Ledger panic), J (T5 Drowning on live TV):** all HOLD. J defeats itself by canon — at T5, static rot melts the cameras.
- **D (Vesper's healing device):** does NOT break the Erosion guardrail — it heals tissue (Bone-adjacent canon), and "no Erosion to the patient" is a red herring (recipients never pay Erosion; the operator does).
- **E (rot-free T3 footage, 50M views):** STRAINED — patched with a fourth case study in 17 §17.4 ("The T3 that didn't rot," membrane-darkness phase, contained by the deepfake dividend).
- **F (BTA discovers the Laggard), G (Arthur internationally wanted):** HOLD — survival is narrow, earned, and not guaranteed. L5-2 (the entity's nature) was leaned on and remains untouched.

## 4. Validation
- 2,603 Markdown cross-references checked across the final folder: **0 broken**.
- ID registry: 0 duplicate definitions; 0 dangling ID references.
- Vector synchronization: all 9 anomaly vectors match 05 §5.5 across files 28/29/01.
- Orphaned-reference scan after the regional rebalance: 0 remaining (the 4 stale references from the Philippines conversion were repaired in v0.7).

## 5. Honest limitations
- The original draft specification called for larger audit volumes (50 audit cycles, 500 questions, 100 faction simulations, 100 cross-system interactions). The real counts are recorded above and in [CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md) — 124 adversarial attacks, 52 findings, 90 reader questions, 10 world simulations, 8 faction audits, 18 cross-system collisions. Depth was prioritized over volume: every finding was patched with a proposed-fix workflow rather than tallied.
- Two coverage blind spots are known and logged in [REMAINING_RISKS.md](REMAINING_RISKS.md): Audit 3 did not re-read files 15 (Education) and 22 (Supernatural Locations) in depth.
- Several containment numbers (Sweeper corps table, clerk sampling math, routine Erosion costs) are newly invented canon, marked CANON and internally consistent — but they have not been independently stress-tested against every downstream use.

# FINAL WORLD AUDIT — THE QUIET TIDE v1.3 (post-forensic-audit)

> **Audit date:** 2026-09-19. **Build:** v1.3 — post-forensic-audit (Albion primary relocation + full forensic patch pass).
> **Authority order:** [01_CANON_MODEL.md](01_CANON_MODEL.md) → [_DECISIONS.md](_DECISIONS.md) → [ISSUES.md](ISSUES.md).
> **Status:** All 123 issues dispositioned. All generated files rebuilt. All programmatic verifications pass.

## 1. Scope and method

- The forensic audit ran in severity order (CRITICAL → HIGH → MEDIUM → LOW) with minimal patching: no new factions, powers, or characters were created; the world was not expanded unnecessarily.
- Every issue resolution — including stale-premise closures and deferrals — carries an exact `[WORLD AUDIT PATCH]` entry in [CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md).
- After each severity tier, affected files were reread and cross-references, IDs, counts, and Markdown links were verified.
- The six intentional mysteries (tide-cycle driver; what is behind the Laggard / whether intelligent; whether the Undertow wants anything; 2018 dip causation; Ilsa Brandt's torn final page; Level-7 Worldtide silence) were preserved — no patch resolved any of them.

## 2. Issue inventory and disposition

| Severity | Count | Patched | Closed (stale premise) | Deferred | Unresolved |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CRITICAL | 3 | 3 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| HIGH | 36 | 33 | 2 | 1 | 0 |
| MEDIUM | 33 | 28 | 2 | 3 | 0 |
| LOW | 51 | 46 | 4 | 1 | 0 |
| **Total** | **123** | **110** | **8** | **5** | **0** |

**Red-team totals:** 570 questions asked · 543 answered from canon · 21 patch-required · 6 intentional mysteries preserved (543 + 21 + 6 = 570).

### CRITICAL (3) — all patched
- **FAC-001 / LAW-001:** The Red Ledger is now a public Code-bound PMC *plus* the Factor's deniable brokerage (the gray half sits outside the treaty framework; the trafficking gap is owned, not papered over).
- **FAC-002:** Private Attuned "armies" reduced to handful-scale combat staff; most private-Attuned personnel are support/noncombat (also resolves ECON-009's staffing arithmetic).
- **LAW-001:** Failed engineered transfer remains *attempted* trafficking; tether succession is legally invisible (no title, no probate, no license category).

### HIGH (36) — all dispositioned
- POWER-001–006 classifications reconciled; Gilded Cradle transfer premise corrected.
- PROTAG-002: Use-7 capacity, failure/corruption behavior, escrow mechanics, weekly handoff, seizure/court limits, and the ~30% failure / ~70% exposure model are now canon.
- ECON-001–011, FAC-003–010, GEO-001, HIST-001–003, SOC-001, NAR-001/002, SER-001, REL-001–003 completed or formally closed.
- COUNTRY-001 = Albion (primary); COUNTRY-005 = United States.
- 22 fully profiled factions; database-only stubs IND-006/007/010 and SUP-011 stay outside the cap; IND-008/009 remain reserved.
- HIST-002 queued for the final database rebuild (done — see §6).

### MEDIUM (33) — all dispositioned
- Person-bound anomalies default to C-A with D-equivalent surveillance.
- Ward Six Lullaby bounded as suggestion seeding, not direct mind control (ruling: *Quiet Desk v. Ward Six Propagation*, 2021 — jurisprudentially adjacent to CASE-006, not part of it).
- "Resonance damage" separated from channeling Erosion (ANOMALY-012/015 Rule sentences corrected in this pass).
- Mandatory relief rotation; 95+ Fathom transfer doctrine; Arthur's use/detection rulers; Use-12 permanent cover loss; Use-10 rot immunity bounded; unfiled mirror phenomenon; Quiet Desk anti-Snowden regime.
- SMD three-arm structure; Aisha dual-hatted; GOV-015/016 deferred (see §4).
- Military anomaly-war doctrine (containment-first, ninety-second Fathom budget, Drowndust last resort, WATCH → SURGE → BLACK TIDE).
- Reed doctrine; EVENT-087's redacted approver (owned plot thread); modern imagery/LLM/Obsidian-recording tech patches; Ravenscroft geography; Shimizu Wound rename; metropolitan amalgamation history; standardized three-minute rule; romance configuration deferred.

### LOW (51) — all dispositioned
- ~60-Fathom career cap, garden leave, pension linkage (POWER-012).
- Escrow deters institutions, not fanatics; engineered-transfer boundary (death-scene positioning voids; employment/housing positioning does not); Use-10 anchor degrades with lag; rival tether-theory schools; 2023 monitor deferred; Membrane/Membranous usage fixed.
- Albion's 1952 accession (not 1949 signatory); SMD Internal Memorandum 19-04; great-power Compact payoffs (US seismograph placement/overflight, China process control, Russia grandfathering).

- Finders-law penalties; static-rot/catalogs distinction; phone-telemetry noise; 2024 concealment principle (interpretation, not observation).
- Depth-pay rotation offset; RC-029 as confidence crisis; Argent ~1:3 reserve ratio + Compact backstop; sector table subtotal ~$118–165B with four named unaccounted lines bridging to the $140–200B CANON total.
- Ferrymen zero-*reported* record; Salt Road escort cost; Care Axis absolution as claim; AT-WAR recodings; TRUCE stance code; Ravenscroft Bay Seep correction; Argent Liwanag as Gray Market hub; CORP-013 Ferrymen Mutual profile; no Compact rehabilitation mechanism; four unrelated Okafors.
- Four-agent field office (2 prefectural examiners); Nagisa Collection front; Takakusa hill station + Calibration Reserve; Karuizawa ~3.5h.
- 1977 revision text in Meridian House; Fukushima Rule two-clause reading; Bell sacred calendar vs Compact dating; Parisada passages THEORY-tagged; kairanban as information-control channel.

## 3. Explicit system stress tests

1. **Transfer-farm attack (FAC-002 / LAW-001):** A faction stages a death scene with a bought witness to harvest the Laggard. Canon: proximate death-scene positioning *voids* the transfer (PROTAG-009); engineered/staged transfers dissipate the tether; the offense remains attempted trafficking. **HOLDS.**
2. **Escrow raid (PROTAG-002 / LOW):** A Compact agency seizes the escrowed uses. Canon: escrow deters institutions, not fanatics; seizure triggers court limits and the exposure model (~70% exposure on failure). **HOLDS.**
3. **Choir mass-seeding (FAC-004 / MEDIUM):** The Drowned Choir propagates the Ward Six Lullaby at scale. Canon: suggestion seeding admits resistance (documented refusers); backlash degrades singers. **HOLDS** (bounded).
4. **Fathom-bankruptcy cascade (ECON-015 / LOW):** Argent faces a run on sounding-denominated accounts. Canon: ~1:3 reserve ratio + implicit Compact backstop; RC-029 is the worked confidence-crisis precedent. **HOLDS.**
5. **Classification arbitrage (POWER-001–006):** A state files a person-bound anomaly as D to dodge C-A surveillance. Canon: person-bound defaults to C-A with D-equivalent surveillance floor. **HOLDS.**
6. **Masquerade breach by scale (SER-001 / TECH-005):** A T5 event in metro Ravenscroft with 10M phone cameras. Canon: three-minute rule + livestream-kill protocols; phone telemetry too noisy for Threshold discrimination; 2024 concealment principle (manage interpretation, not observation). **HOLDS.**
7. **Ninth Bell cell vs Peal (MIL-004):** A tactical cell triggers the Peal protocol early. Canon: cells are tactical, the Peal is strategic; WATCH → SURGE → BLACK TIDE escalation ladder. **HOLDS.**
8. **Treaty-lawyer attack (POL-003):** A signatory challenges Albion's standing. Canon: Albion acceded 1952 via US-backed quiet arrangement; not a 1949 signatory. **HOLDS.**
9. **Resonance-damage compensation fraud (MEDIUM):** A claimant files Erosion as Saint-inflicted damage. Canon: resonance damage is inflicted (I-flagged, Meridian Re-eligible); Erosion is self-incurred and non-compensable. **HOLDS.**
10. **Database-consistency attack (this audit):** Every ID referenced in canon resolves. Verified programmatically: 412/412 in 35_CANON_DATABASE.md; CG-001..052 resolve to 31_CONFLICT_ENGINE.md; sole exception CRIM-008 is a deliberate reserved-namespace marker. **HOLDS.**

## 4. Deferred issues (explicit, with reasons)

1. **Romance configuration** — deferred to serialization planning, before chapter 1 (authorial choice, not a canon gap).
2. **2023 monitor** — deferred via austerity backlog (resource constraint, re-check scheduled).
3. **GOV-015/016 stubs** — deferred; recorded, not expanded (minimal-patching mandate).
4. **HIGH-tier deferral (1)** — recorded in the HIGH changelog batch; rationale in [CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md).
5. **MEDIUM-tier deferral (3)** — recorded in the MEDIUM changelog batch; rationale in [CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md).

## 5. Remaining risks

- **Generated-file staleness:** mitigated — 35_CANON_DATABASE.md rebuilt, all six DATABASE/ views regenerated, 37_FINAL_WORLD_BIBLE.md regenerated (v1.3).
- **Interpretive risk:** the TRUCE stance code and AT-WAR recodings are new controlled vocabulary; future writers must use the legend in 34, not intuition.
- **Theory-tag discipline:** Bell chronology, Parisada passages, and Membrane Theory are in-world theories; serialization must not upgrade them to CANON without a Tribunal-grade event.
- **The six intentional mysteries** remain load-bearing unknowns — any future patch that resolves one must be flagged as a canon event, not a fix.

## CRITICAL

### LAW-001 — CRITICAL — The Factor's Laggard brokering is an unrecognized trafficking crime, and the real transfer mechanism sits outside every statute
**Merges:** FAC-002 (CRITICAL) · LAW-001 (Moderate) · LAW-002 (Moderate)
**Location:** `25_TIMEmessaging app.md` (EVENT-093) · `31_CONFLICT_ENGINE.md` (RC-043) · `14_LAW_ENFORCEMENT.md` §14.3(2) · `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.8
**Conflicting statements:**
- 25 (EVENT-093): "The Red Ledger's Factor (CHAR-027) circulated a quiet inquiry: *'Persistent Retrograde Shadow, Ravenscroft, T1'* — a buyer wants the Laggard." / "Consequence: ANOMALY-001 is on the market. Arthur doesn't know."
- 31 (RC-043): "The Factor (CHAR-027) reveals the buyer behind the Laggard inquiry ([EVENT-093](25_TIMEmessaging app.md)): a Pale Court cadet branch, bidding for a wedding gift."
- 14: "**Person-attached anomalies cannot be Ledger-titled** — a tether like the Laggard ([ANOMALY-001](28_ANOMALIES.md)) is not ownable property: the holder retains full personhood, and **any 'sale' of the anomaly is in law a sale *of the holder*, prosecuted as trafficking** under Accords law."
- 12: "The Ledger refuses anomaly-trafficking contracts (Code: 'we're not couriers for things that eat couriers')."
- 29: "**Transfer cannot be engineered.** If the holder's death or Drowning is staged, assisted, or arranged to deliver the tether — or if the nearest Loud witness was *positioned* rather than present — the transfer fails and the tether **dissipates**." / "the tether passes to the **nearest Loud witness**… not inheritance, not worthiness, not fate."
**Why it is a problem:** Three stacked failures. (1) The novel's inciting market transaction is, under the bible's own law, **trafficking in a person** — and no file treats it as trafficking. It simultaneously violates the Ledger's Code (per 12) and Accords law (per 14), and is strategically irrational for a broker whose business depends on Compact tolerance: the inquiry is *circulated*, not compartmentalized. (2) The trafficking statute's premise misdescribes its own example: the Laggard cannot be transferred even in principle — the physics voids every transaction the law criminalizes — so the offense is necessarily *attempted* trafficking / crimes against the holder, which the statute never says. (3) The *real* transfer mechanism (death-proximity to the nearest Loud witness) sits in a complete legal vacuum: no title, no probate (CASE-004 excludes non-property), no license category, no recipient reporting duty. The trafficking prohibition guards a door while the tether walks through the wall. Resolution is gated on decision (a) — SUP-004's identity — since the Factor's conduct is impossible under the PMC reading and legally suicidal under the gray-market reading.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Reframe the inquiry as explicitly *non-purchase*: the buyer seeks observation rights, data, or a futures-style first-refusal — still gray, but not trafficking (works under either SUP-004 identity).
2. Keep it as trafficking and make the illegality the point: the Factor is compromised, the Pale Court cadet branch is rogue, and the Compact's response becomes a plot engine — but then the files must *recognize* it as trafficking.
3. Downgrade EVENT-093's classification from a circulated Compact-attributed inquiry to a deniable cutout chain, giving the Factor insulation.
4. Add one acknowledgment clause in 14 §14.3(2): non-severable tether succession is *legally invisible*; the Division's C-A filing is an administrative convenience, not a legal regime (see decision (c) — do not invent a tether-succession statute here).

### FAC-001 — CRITICAL — SUP-004 is two irreconcilable factions
**Merges:** FAC-001 (Critical)
**Location:** `12_FACTIONS.md` (SUP-004 profile) vs `14_LAW_ENFORCEMENT.md`, `24_HISTORY.md`, `25_TIMEmessaging app.md` (EVENT-084, EVENT-093), `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md`, `31_CONFLICT_ENGINE.md` (RC-017, RC-043, CG-048), `35_CANON_DATABASE.md`
**Conflicting statements:**
- 12: "**Ideology:** **The Code.** (1) No Choir work... (2) No Drowndust, no infohazards, no children. (3) The contract is the contract — but a contract that requires breaking the Code is void, and the Ledger returns the fee *with interest* and blacklists the client." / "The Ledger refuses anomaly-trafficking contracts."
- 35: "| SUP-004 | The Red Ledger | Gray-market auctioneers; the Factor (CHAR-027) |"
- 14: "the Red Ledger exploits the variance, which is why its Factor keeps a buyer list for things that cannot legally be bought."
- 24: "The Red Ledger (SUP-004) auctioned a T4 Object Seep's *Rule* in Trieste (2021) ([EVENT-084](25_TIMEmessaging app.md))."
- 25 (EVENT-093): "The Red Ledger's Factor (CHAR-027) circulated a quiet inquiry: *'Persistent Retrograde Shadow, Ravenscroft, T1'* — a buyer wants the Laggard."
**Why it is a problem:** Six files describe a gray-market auction house whose Factor brokers person-attached anomalies; one file (12) describes a Code-bound mercenary company that refuses trafficking contracts, returns fees with interest, and hunts Erosion traffickers kill-on-sight. The Code as written *forbids* the Factor's entire business. Every downstream question — strategy, economy (ECON-009), the Factor's inquiry — is unanswerable until one identity is selected. This is the single largest canon break in the bible, sitting on the novel's central market thread (EVENT-093 → RC-043 → CG-048).
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Keep the 12 PMC and move the Factor/auctions to a new or existing gray-market actor (the Ash Exchange already runs auctions) — requires rewriting EVENT-084/093/RC-017/RC-043/CG-048 attributions.
2. Keep the gray-market auctioneer (six files vs. one) and rewrite the 12 profile as the Ledger's *cover*: the Code is real for the PMC division while the Factor runs a black-book brokerage the Partners don't acknowledge (12 already gives the Ledger a "black book" of Code-violating clients — the infrastructure exists).
3. Split SUP-004 into two registry entries. **Coordinator decision (a) in `_DECISIONS.md` — no patch until picked.**

### FAC-002 — CRITICAL — Five factions field private Attuned forces in violation of a CANON hard rule
**Merges:** FAC-003 (Critical)
**Location:** `08_ECONOMY.md` §8.3 (CANON rule) · `33_MYSTERIES.md` ("NOT mysterious" #2) · `12_FACTIONS.md` (SUP-001, SUP-004) · `11_CORPORATIONS.md` (CORP-001) · `12_FACTIONS.md` (CRIM-002, CRIM-006)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 08: "> CANON: No corporation, syndicate, or cult fields a private force of more than a handful of combat-licensed Attuned."
- 33: "**Why there are no Attuned armies.** Four mass-Awakening attempts, four piles of the Drowned ([EVENT-028](25_TIMEmessaging app.md), [04_POWER_SYSTEM.md](04_POWER_SYSTEM.md)). CANON. Not a mystery — a morgue."
- 12 (SUP-004): "~2,400 active operators (70% Attuned, T1–T4...)" — ~1,680 combat Attuned. 12 (SUP-001): 300 Attuned Tideguard. 11 (CORP-001): "~400 licensed Attuned (mostly T2, some T3)." 12 (CRIM-002): 80 Attuned enforcers; (CRIM-006): 40 Attuned.
**Why it is a problem:** A CANON rule with five numbered exceptions — the largest (1,680) exceeding most national threshold establishments. "A handful" cannot stretch to 1,680, 400, or 300. ECON-009 shows the Ledger can't even afford its force at $900M turnover ($375k/operator vs. the bible's own $400k–1M fully-loaded T3 cost).
**Proposed solutions:**
1. **Enforce the rule:** cut forces to handful-scale — the Ledger becomes an elite cadre, the Tideguard a ceremonial/close-protection unit, Halcyon's 400 explicitly *support* personnel not combat-licensed.
2. **Amend the rule:** the CANON becomes "no private force above a handful *without a Compact charter*," with each violator holding a named, revocable, inspected charter — converting the bug into five plot hooks.
3. Reclassify most Attuned as non-combat-licensed (medics, Lanterns, counselors) so *combat-licensed* counts stay handful-scale. The rule or the rosters must change; both standing is not an option.

## HIGH

### POWER-001 — HIGH — ANOMALY-006: ratified `C-C` vs catalog prose `D`
**Merges:** POWER-001 (HIGH)
**Location:** `05_ANOMALY_CLASSIFICATION.md:96` · `28_ANOMALIES.md` ANOMALY-006 status line · `33_MYSTERIES.md` L4-2
**Conflicting statements:**
- 05: "ANOMALY-006 'The Thursday Rain' — `MV-T3/C-C/R/Regional/Reactive`."
- 28: "STATUS: **D** (downgraded 2002 following the JCC's Regional Containment Doctrine revision)."
- 33: "**L4-2** — *Where is the Thursday Rain this year?* A downgraded threat, currently reclassified."
**Why it is a problem:** The JCC cannot have *downgraded* a Compact-ratified C-class filing to D — under 05.4 only the Classification Office can revise a vector on re-examination; a regional bureau can lower posture, not classification. 33 also treats the anomaly as currently mobile "reclassified" — but the Rule is *nine fixed Thursdays a year*, not a moving target.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Treat 05's vector as authoritative (Compact ratification outranks field catalogs; 05.5:95 "Vectors shown are Compact-ratified unless noted") and rewrite 28's status line as a posture adjustment, not a downgrade (see decision (h)).
2. Treat 28's field truth as correct and re-file the vector as `C-D` in 05.
3. Per-case rule (05 wins except where 28's field truth is load-bearing) with an explicit Classification Office dispute note per 05.3 — see `_DECISIONS.md` (h) for the full recommendation.

### POWER-002 — HIGH — ANOMALY-012: ratified `C-D` vs catalog prose `E`
**Merges:** POWER-002 (HIGH)
**Location:** `05_ANOMALY_CLASSIFICATION.md:101` · `28_ANOMALIES.md` ANOMALY-012 status line
**Conflicting statements:**
- 05: "ANOMALY-012 'The Static Saint' — `MV-T5/C-D/I+M/Memetic/Sapient`."
- 28: "STATUS: **E** — 'The Seismograph Authority proposed an **E** containment classification in 2022 (uncontained, uncontainable) but the JCC refused to file it — 'you don't rate what you can't bound.'"
**Why it is a problem:** The JCC's refusal is treated as a win over the Seismograph Authority, but the refusal may be *illegal* — the Authority, not the JCC, is the designated filing authority for C-class-and-above review per 07. The 05 vector is the ratified record; 28's prose describes an open interagency fight, not a settled classification.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Keep `C-D` as the ratified vector and mark 28's `E` proposal as an unfiled dispute note with the Classification Office, not a status.
2. File the `E` and accept the containment implications (a formal `E` filing triggers evacuation-priority doctrine — heavier rewrite).
3. Per-case rule per decision (h): keep `C-D`, annotate the Seismograph Authority's disagreement in 05.3.

### POWER-003 — HIGH — ANOMALY-013: ratified `C-B` vs catalog prose `D`
**Merges:** POWER-003 (HIGH)
**Location:** `05_ANOMALY_CLASSIFICATION.md:102` · `28_ANOMALIES.md` ANOMALY-013 status line
**Conflicting statements:**
- 05: "ANOMALY-013 'The Salt Garden' — `MV-T4/C-B/R/Urban/Reactive`."
- 28: "STATUS: **D** (post-2014 review downgraded the **containment** classification from C — the garden *cannot* be sealed, only visited — but the threat class remains B)."
**Why it is a problem:** 28's sentence is the textbook case of the thread/threat orthogonality doctrine (05.6 cites it) — "cannot be sealed" is *why* it's B-class: threat and containment are orthogonal, and downgrading the containment axis while "the threat class remains B" misunderstands the vector: C means *regional* scope of effort, and nine years of standing containment *is* regional-scope effort.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Keep `C-B` (the teaching example is load-bearing for the whole classification philosophy) and rewrite 28's parenthetical as "cannot be vaulted, only visited."
2. Treat the downgrade as real and re-file in 05 as `C-D`, at the cost of the teaching example.
3. Per-case rule per decision (h).

### POWER-004 — HIGH — ANOMALY-030: ratified `C-C` vs catalog prose `D`
**Merges:** POWER-004 (HIGH)
**Location:** `05_ANOMALY_CLASSIFICATION.md:106` · `28_ANOMALIES.md` ANOMALY-030 status line
**Conflicting statements:**
- 05: "ANOMALY-030 'The Second Silence Archive' — `MV-T5/C-C/I/Memetic/Sapient`."
- 28: "STATUS: **D** — 'Filed **D** under the 1996 Archivists' Charter: 'a Sapient anomaly that cooperates is not a containment problem, it is a *custody* problem.'"
**Why it is a problem:** The Charter is a compact document; it cannot override a Compact ratification. `C-C` is the ratified vector; `D` is a custodian's filing argument. Worse, the `D` filing would *remove* the Compact's funding for the expensive rotation protocol 28 itself describes.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Keep `C-C` and rewrite the Charter's 1996 filing as the Archivists' *proposed* reading, rejected or superseded — preserving the Compact funding the containment needs.
2. Formalize the custody-vs-containment distinction as a new filing axis, which requires touching 05.2's whole vector grammar.
3. Per-case rule per decision (h).

### POWER-005 — HIGH — The Gilded Cradle has two incompatible Rules
**Merges:** POWER-006 (HIGH)
**Location:** `28_ANOMALIES.md` ANOMALY-008 vs `30_SCENARIO_ARCHIVE.md` Scenario 3 ("The Gilded Cradle") · `35_CANON_DATABASE.md`
**Conflicting statements:**
- 28: "**STATUS: C-C/R+P/Local/Reactive** — **CANON: Only eleven cradles are active.** A twelfth use triggers the Tribunal's standing prohibition order… The eleven children cannot age, cannot leave, cannot Drown — eleven Fathom-debts the world is carrying… *The Order's position*: the debt is owed to the Tide and cannot be forgiven; only transferred. *The Cartographers' position*: find the original eleven debt-holders…"
- 30: "**Status: `C-B/R/Urban/Reactive`** — 'The cradle does not merely *hold* children — it *keeps what is placed in it*: objects, memories, oaths. The Tribunal's exception clause permits Sweepers to deposit contaminated equipment inside.'"
- 35: "ANOMALY-008 | C-B/R/Urban | An object that 'keeps what is placed in it'"
**Why it is a problem:** Three different statuses, two different Rules: an Erosion-transfer cradle with eleven children vs. an object that preserves whatever is placed in it. The two Rules cannot both be true of one anomaly — they generate different threats, different containment, different stories. (The workers' alternative — a staged two-phase Rule, or one Rule lying — would be a retcon, not a clarification.)
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Keep 28's Erosion-transfer Rule and rewrite 30's Scenario 3 around it (Sweepers responding to an unauthorized twelfth use — arguably a better scenario than the exception clause).
2. Keep 30/35's "keeps what is placed in it" and rewrite 28's entry (loses the eleven children, the trolley problem, and four factions' positions — the most expensive option).
3. Split into a new ANOMALY-031 — but 28 claims 002–030 complete, so a split needs the registry-completeness note amended too. **Decision (f) — no patch until picked.**

### PROTAG-002 — HIGH — The dead-man's-switch escrow has no mechanism, no limits, and no reliability model
**Merges:** SER-001 (Moderate) · RED-003 (High) · RED-013 (High) · RED-020 (High)
**Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §§B.11, C.9, D.7, B.10 · `27_SERIALIZATION_GUIDE.md` §27.8
**Conflicting statements:**
- 29 (C.9): "The **Dead-Man's-Switch Protocol**: should Arthur die or be taken, his pre-written cache drops — everything he knows about the tether, the Compact's Quiet ledgers, the escrowed evidence against the Cartographers' Quiet Desk, his full Use-log with **Fathom tallies**… A dead man's switch only deters **rational** actors. The Cartographers' Quiet Desk is rational. NQA's black desk is rational. So is everyone else with a filing cabinet." / "**The cache updates itself.** Every new Use-entry he writes syncs to the dead man's switch automatically — a dead man's switch that *does* require maintenance isn't a switch, it's a diary."
- 29 (B.10): "his full Use-log with **Fathom tallies**… **Every line-itemized Erosion event** — the Compact's own Quiet ledgers don't record what Arthur's cache records. Publishing it would hand every Actuary in the Compact the dataset they've been begging for."
- 29 (D.7): "He keeps a lawyer's letter in a safety-deposit box."
- RED-013 worker finding: "Use 7 (shadow storage) also lacks storage limits — a shadow-archive with no capacity ceiling or failure mode."
**Why it is a problem:** The novel's primary deterrent is a handwave: no mechanism (who holds it, how the cache "updates itself" without Use 7, what Use 7 does during the update — Use 7 is shadow-*storage*, not shadow-*transmission*), no limits (what if the escrow is discovered, subpoenaed, or seized — a lawyer's safety-deposit box can be opened by a court order), and no reliability model for the only actors it must deter (the Quiet Desk is rational — rational actors discount threats they can verify as bluffs). Separately, Use 7's shadow storage needs a capacity ceiling and failure mode, since it backs the escrow. The task's note is accepted: RED-013 is Use-7 storage limits, not the dead-man's-switch — it is merged here only because Use 7 backs the escrow mechanism.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Name the mechanism: the escrow is a lawyer-held sealed affidavit with a standing instruction triggered by a missed check-in, plus a dead-man's *publication* split across two Loud journalists — redundancy for the "seized box" case, and a stated failure rate.
2. Make the unreliability load-bearing: the Quiet Desk *knows* the switch has a ~30% chance of failing (seizure, corruption, a missed check-in while he's in the Membranous phase) — the deterrence works through uncertainty, and a mid-series arc can be built on the switch being tested.
3. State Use 7's ceiling and failure mode explicitly (capacity in "nights of record," failure = the archive becomes read-only/partially corrupted), and route the cache's auto-sync through a stated mechanism.

### ECON-001 — HIGH — Meridian Re's supernatural premium arithmetic breaks the whole insurance canon
**Merges:** ECON-001 (Major)
**Location:** `08_ECONOMY.md` §8.2 · `19_TECHNOLOGY.md` (cat bond pricing) · `18_FINANCE.md`
**Conflicting statements:**
- 08: the anomaly-insurance line is sized at "$28–35B" in the summary and in the Meridian Re premium pool; the worker computed Meridian Re's implied premium at roughly **9% of the whole $140–200B supernatural-adjacent economy**, which the worker judged implausible for a reinsurer's book.
- 19: cat bond pricing implies a ~6% yield on a very small issuance base, inconsistent with the $28–35B premium pool.
**Why it is a problem:** The flagship CANON market numbers don't reconcile: the premium pool, the economy share, and the cat-bond pricing imply three different loss models. Every downstream use of the Meridian Re numbers (RC-027 pricing, the reinsurance-spiral plot) inherits the break.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Re-size the pool: publish a CANON loss triangle (frequency × severity) and back-solve the premium to ~2–4% of the supernatural-adjacent economy.
2. Keep the $28–35B but shrink the economy denominator — no, the $140–200B is CANON-stated; instead fix the cat-bond side: the bonds are priced on a *different* (higher) loss layer.
3. Downgrade the cat-bond pricing example from a priced instrument to a *proposed* one that failed to clear.

### ECON-002 — HIGH — RC-026 has no payer waterfall
**Merges:** ECON-002 (Major)
**Location:** `31_CONFLICT_ENGINE.md` RC-026
**Conflicting statements:**
- 31 (RC-026): "The Ravenscroft reinsurance renewal (RC-027) is priced **before** the loss year closes… the Compact backstops the renewal to keep the Veil intact" — vs 31 (RC-029): the bank-run RC where "the backstop is a rumor."
- 08: "> CANON: The Compact backstops the insurance market. This is the 'reinsurance spiral': every crisis makes the Compact the insurer of last resort."
**Why it is a problem:** RC-026 is staged as the Ravenscroft renewal crisis, but no file says who pays first, who pays second, and where the Compact's backstop attaches. The reinsurance spiral is a CANON mechanism with no attachment point — the plot's money can't move.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Publish the waterfall in 08 or 31: primary carriers → Meridian Re pool → national guarantee (Albion) → Compact backstop, with the yen/rupiah split stated.
2. Keep the ambiguity but mark it in-world: the missing waterfall is *why* RC-026 is a crisis (the attachment is disputed), not a canon gap.
3. Move the crisis to a different trigger (capacity withdrawal rather than attachment dispute).

### ECON-003 — HIGH — Service-denominated Quiet claims vs "NQA pays claims in rupiah"
**Merges:** ECON-003 (Major)
**Location:** `08_ECONOMY.md` §8.9 · `19_TECHNOLOGY.md` §19.9
**Conflicting statements:**
- 08: Quiet claims are service-denominated (favor-trading, not cash).
- 19: "NQA pays claims in rupiah" — a cash settlement statement.
**Why it is a problem:** The bible's central economic thesis is that the Quiet economy runs on obligations, not currency. A cash-denominated claim settlement in rupiah contradicts the thesis and creates a fiat-conversion event the firewall rules don't cover.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Keep service-denomination as CANON and rewrite 19's line: NQA *advances* rupiah as a bridge loan against a service-denominated claim.
2. Carve out Indonesia: NQA's older system is cash-denominated, Albion's is service-denominated — and the friction between them becomes a plot point.
3. Define the fiat firewall's conversion protocol and route the rupiah payment through it (then ECON-006's firewall gap must be fixed first).

### ECON-004 — HIGH — Anti-fraud is claimant-side only; the system can't catch the real threat
**Merges:** ECON-004 (Major)
**Location:** `08_ECONOMY.md` §8.5
**Conflicting statements:**
- 08: anti-fraud machinery targets *claimant* fraud (staged seeps, inflated losses) — "priced into the loss ratio."
- 08: the actual documented frauds in the bible (EVENT-084's auctioned Rule, the Factor's inquiry, Meridian Re's book) are all *underwriter/broker*-side.
**Why it is a problem:** The fraud model polices the wrong side of the market. Every staged beat in the bible (the Trieste auction, the Laggard inquiry) sails through a system designed to catch inflated roof-damage claims.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Add broker/underwriter-side fraud doctrine to 08: the Compact's real exposure is mispriced risk and misfiled vectors (which also fixes the classification-fraud incentive in POWER-007).
2. Keep claimant-side focus but make the blind spot load-bearing: the Compact *knows* it can't police brokers and prices the ignorance in — the Meridian Re pool carries an explicit fraud loading.
3. Move the fraud beats into the Gray Market, where no anti-fraud machinery claims to operate.

### ECON-005 — HIGH — The sounding numeraire is undefined
**Merges:** ECON-005 (Major)
**Location:** `08_ECONOMY.md` §8.6 · `17_MEDIA_SOCIETY.md` (sounding rates $12–16k)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 08: soundings are the price-discovery mechanism for the whole anomaly economy — quoted in service units like "One T2 object containment-week."
- 17: sounding rates are quoted in dollars ($12–16k) while 08 prices Quiet obligations in services.
**Why it is a problem:** The economy's central price signal has no stated unit of account. If soundings are cash-priced, the fiat firewall (ECON-006) applies to the most important market in the bible and it currently doesn't.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Define the numeraire: soundings are priced in *Compact drawing rights* (a unit of account, not circulating currency), with a published dollar reference rate.
2. Keep dollar pricing and route soundings through the fiat firewall explicitly.
3. Make soundings service-denominated like Quiet claims (slower, but consistent).

### ECON-006 — HIGH — The fiat firewall has no defined leak
**Merges:** ECON-006 (Major)
**Location:** `08_ECONOMY.md` §8.4 · `11_CORPORATIONS.md` (Kurogane)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 08: the fiat firewall separates Quiet money from ordinary money — a CANON mechanism.
- 11: Kurogane's "grown" materials are sold into ordinary supply chains (ECON-008's finding), which is a firewall crossing with no stated protocol.
**Why it is a problem:** The firewall is asserted but never shown working: no file describes what happens at the boundary — who converts, at what rate, with what reporting. Every crossover beat (Kurogane sales, rupiah claims) is an unexamined exception.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Write the firewall protocol: licensed converters, a published rate, mandatory Seismograph Authority reporting — one subsection in 08.
2. Keep the firewall as doctrine and make the crossings the crime: Kurogane's sales are a *violation*, not a feature (feeds the Kurogane audit thread).
3. Downgrade the firewall from CANON mechanism to in-world *claim* (the Compact says it works; the Gray Market knows it doesn't).

### ECON-007 — HIGH — Provenance price formation is handwaved
**Merges:** ECON-007 (Major)
**Location:** `08_ECONOMY.md` §8.7
**Conflicting statements:**
- 08: provenance (a documented Quiet history) commands a premium — "Every high-value transaction is a bespoke auction or a bespoke contract," and the Trieste auction (EVENT-084) is the worked example; authentication is "vaulting, authentication (Lantern-verified Rule documentation), and collateralized lending against assessed sounding value."
- 08: no file states *how* Lantern-verified provenance is itself verified — so the premium has no mechanism: in a market full of forgers, an unverifiable premium is a fraud engine (cf. ECON-004).
**Why it is a problem:** The bible's most valuable intangible has no authentication mechanism. The Trieste auction's price can't be trusted by the reader or by the market.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Give the Compact a provenance registry (Lantern-examined, seal-chained) — the registry itself becomes a plot target.
2. Make provenance explicitly *unverifiable* and price it as reputation: the premium is a social fact, and the frauds around it are the story (pairs with ECON-004 option 2).
3. Restrict provenance premiums to the Gray Market and keep the Compact out of it.

### ECON-008 — HIGH — Kurogane's "grown" anomalous materials threaten the non-fungibility CANON
**Merges:** ECON-008 (Major)
**Location:** `11_CORPORATIONS.md` (CORP-001) · `02_WORLD_RULES.md` (CANON: anomalies are non-fungible)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 02: "Anomalies are non-fungible." (CANON-LOCKED)
- 11: Kurogane "grows" anomalous materials at industrial repeatability — the worker's finding: repeatable production of anomalous *material* reads as fungibility.
**Why it is a problem:** If Kurogane can manufacture anomalous matter on a production line, the non-fungibility rule — a load-bearing CANON constraint — is broken, and the whole scarcity economy collapses.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Cap it: Kurogane grows *resonant* materials (Tide-touched, not true anomalies) — useful, scarce, but not anomalies; the non-fungibility rule survives.
2. Make the growth a CANON-LOCKED exception with a CHANGELOG entry (the rule change protocol exists for this) — expensive but honest.
3. Make Kurogane's process a fraud: the "growth" is laundering (recovered materials re-certified), and the audit thread is the story.

### ECON-009 — HIGH — The Red Ledger's $900M turnover cannot fund 2,400 operators
**Merges:** ECON-009 (Major)
**Location:** `08_ECONOMY.md` §8.3 · `12_FACTIONS.md` (SUP-004)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 12: "~2,400 active operators (70% Attuned, T1–T4…)" with fully-loaded costs the bible itself prices at $400k–1M per T3 operator-year.
- 08: the Ledger's turnover is ~$900M — $375k per operator before overhead, safehouses, Drowndust insurance, and the Factor's cut.
**Why it is a problem:** The bible's own cost model bankrupts its largest private faction. (Interacts with FAC-002: if the force is cut to handful-scale per the CANON rule, the arithmetic heals automatically.)
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Fix it via FAC-002 option 1 (enforce the handful rule): a small elite cadre is affordable at $900M.
2. Keep the force and fix the turnover: the Ledger's *brokerage* volume (not turnover) is an order of magnitude larger — restate the $900M as net revenue on a much larger flow.
3. Make the insolvency load-bearing: the Ledger is overextended, which is *why* the Factor is brokering the Laggard (desperation pricing) — pairs with LAW-001 option 2.

### ECON-010 — HIGH — The Ravenscroft berth concession has no payment waterfall
**Merges:** ECON-012 (Major)
**Location:** `08_ECONOMY.md` §8.10 · `20_GEOGRAPHY.md` (Ravenscroft port)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 08: the berth concession is a major Quiet revenue instrument.
- 12: "**Resources:** Ravenscroft's container berths — controlled through licensed front firms (stevedoring, warehousing, customs brokerage)."
- 20: the port's control arrangements are described (Blackwater Syndicate truce, SMD port protocol) but no file states who pays whom, in what order, for berth rights.
**Why it is a problem:** The waterfront is the novel's primary physical stage and a revenue instrument with no revenue mechanics — the Blackwater Syndicate truce (06) has no price.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Publish the waterfall: concession fees → port authority → SMD containment levy → Blackwater Syndicate "cooperation" payments, with the Quiet/ordinary split stated.
2. Keep the price unstated but mark it as the truce's *secret term* — the one number both sides lie about (plot hook).
3. Move the concession revenue offstage: it's ordinary-economy money and the Quiet books don't see it.

### ECON-011 — HIGH — Borrowed gold can survive long enough to clear T+2
**Merges:** ECON-013 (Major)
**Location:** `02_WORLD_RULES.md` (borrowed matter reverts within 72 hours) · `08_ECONOMY.md` §8.11
**Conflicting statements:**
- 02: "No permanent created matter; borrowed matter reverts within 72 hours." (CANON-LOCKED)
- 08: settlement is T+2 — 48 hours — which fits *inside* the 72-hour reversion window.
**Why it is a problem:** Borrowed gold can be delivered, sold, and settled before it reverts. The 72-hour rule does not prevent financial fraud at T+2 — the whole "borrowed wealth reverts" safeguard is a dead letter for securities settlement.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Add a seasoning rule: Quiet-adjacent settlement requires T+5 (outside the window) or delivery-versus-payment with a 72-hour clawback.
2. Make the reversion *traceable*: reverted gold leaves a forensic signature and the Compact prosecutes the attempt — deterrence replaces prevention.
3. Shorten the reversion window for precious metals (a CANON change requiring the CHANGELOG protocol — expensive; prefer 1 or 2).

### FAC-003 — HIGH — CRIM-002 has two leaders
**Merges:** FAC-008 (Major)
**Location:** `12_FACTIONS.md` (CRIM-002) · `25_TIMEmessaging app.md` (EVENT-091)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 12: "CRIM-002 … **Led by** a council of three dons, first among equals **Hendra Kusuma**…"
- 25 (EVENT-091): "…the Blackwater Syndicate's **oyabun, Dewi Sartika**, signed the port protocol…"
**Why it is a problem:** Two incompatible leadership structures for the same syndicate, in the file that stages its signature event. The port protocol's signatory must exist.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Split the roles: Dewi Sartika is the titular oyabun (ceremonial signatory), Hendra Kusuma runs operations — the council-of-three becomes the *wakashu* council under a figurehead.
2. Pick one: Dewi as oyabun, rewrite the council; or Hendra as first-among-equals, rewrite EVENT-091's signatory.
3. Make the contradiction the story: a succession dispute is in progress during EVENT-091 (but then the timeline must show it).

### FAC-004 — HIGH — CORP-001's identity is split between two companies
**Merges:** FAC-009 (Major)
**Location:** `11_CORPORATIONS.md` (CORP-001) · `25_TIMEmessaging app.md` (EVENT-090)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 11: CORP-001 is Halcyon — "a Singapore-headquartered **private military and logistics company**… ~400 licensed Attuned."
- 25 (EVENT-090): "…**Halcyon Data Systems**, the Singaporean **data-analytics** firm, signed the Meridian Re modeling contract…"
**Why it is a problem:** A PMC and a data-analytics firm share one registry ID. The Meridian Re modeling contract's counterparty is indeterminate.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Make them one company: Halcyon is a PMC *with* a data-analytics division (the modeling contract is the analytics arm's work) — one profile, two business lines.
2. Split the registry: CORP-001 (Halcyon PMC) and a new CORP entry for Halcyon Data Systems as a subsidiary or namesake.
3. Rename one: the analytics firm becomes a different Singaporean company.

### FAC-005 — HIGH — Santara's total ignorance contradicts the seeding-desk incident
**Merges:** FAC-013 (Major)
**Location:** `12_FACTIONS.md` (SUP-001, Tideguard) · `25_TIMEmessaging app.md` (EVENT-088)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 12: Santara "…**knows nothing** about the Tide, the Compact, or the Quiet…"
- 25 (EVENT-088): "…a Santara **seeding-desk analyst** flagged the Ravenscroft pressure anomaly *before* the Seismograph Authority's bulletin…"
**Why it is a problem:** A total-ignorance statement is falsified by a single counterexample — and the counterexample is staged in the timeline. The worker's finding stands: Santara cannot both know nothing and have a seeding desk that detects pressure anomalies.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Move the incident: the analyst flagged it *without* understanding it (a statistical anomaly in shipping data), and Santara's ignorance survives — the flag was noise to them, signal to the reader.
2. Reframe: the seeding desk is a Quiet cutout *inside* Santara, and the "knows nothing" line describes the parent company's leadership — narrower statement, same effect.
3. Delete the incident (but it feeds the Ravenscroft pressure plot — prefer 1).

### FAC-006 — HIGH — Twelve faction knowledge states are stale post-relocation
**Merges:** FAC-015 (Major)
**Location:** `12_FACTIONS.md` (SUP-001, SUP-002, SUP-003, SUP-004, SUP-006, IND-001, IND-002, IND-003, IND-005, CRIM-002, CRIM-004, CRIM-006)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 12: "…twelve factions' knowledge states still describe pre-relocation (Jakarta-primary) facts…"
**Why it is a problem:** A dozen knowledge-state fields describe a world that no longer exists (Jakarta-primary). Every cross-file reference to what these factions know is unreliable.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Rewrite the twelve knowledge states for the Albion-primary world (mechanical pass, low risk).
2. Downgrade the knowledge states from CANON to *reported* (in-world intelligence estimates, explicitly fallible) — cheaper, and consistent with 26's information doctrine.
3. Delete the knowledge-state fields and let knowledge be shown, not told.

### FAC-007 — HIGH — The 36-entry registry has only 22 profiles
**Merges:** FAC-016 (Major)
**Location:** `12_FACTIONS.md` · `34_CONFLICT_MATRIX.md`
**Conflicting statements:**
- 12: the registry is numbered for 36 factions; only 22 have profiles (SUP-011, IND-006/007/010, REL-008, IND-008/009 are stubs or missing).
- 34: "34_CONFLICT_MATRIX.md's header claims 36 but covers 22."
**Why it is a problem:** The registry's numbering promises 36 actors; 14 are ghosts. Cross-references to stub IDs (notably REL-008, which the conflict engine already stages at 31:513) dangle.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Reduce the registry to the 22 profiled factions and re-tag the rest as reserved namespaces (see decision (b)).
2. Write the missing profiles — five full profiles plus stubs, with regression risk on every new fact.
3. Minimum: fix 34's header to 22 and delist the stubs from the registry count. **Decision (b) — no patch until picked.**

### FAC-008 — HIGH — The NQA's flagged-applicant hiring thread must pay off or be reclassified
**Merges:** FAC-014 (Major)
**Location:** `12_FACTIONS.md` (NQA) · `25_TIMEmessaging app.md` (EVENT-087)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 12: "…the NQA's Quiet hiring apparatus **hired a flagged applicant** in 2022 ([EVENT-087](25_TIMEmessaging app.md))…"
- 25 (EVENT-087): "…a flagged applicant was hired — **PROVISIONAL**, approver redacted…"
**Why it is a problem:** A flagged applicant hired by a Quiet apparatus with a redacted approver is either the novel's central infiltration thread or a continuity bug. It cannot sit as an unexplained fact.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Register it as a plot thread with a named owner in 31 (the approver is redacted *in-world*, not from the author) — pairs with decision (d)'s SSW fix.
2. Reclassify as a bug and rewrite: the applicant was flagged *after* hiring (routine post-hire review), no infiltration implied.
3. Pay it off immediately in the bible: name the approver and the faction behind the hire.

### FAC-009 — HIGH — The SMD archive "winning move" is stated but never staged
**Merges:** FAC-017 (Major)
**Location:** `06_GOVERNMENTS.md` (SMD) · `31_CONFLICT_ENGINE.md`
**Conflicting statements:**
- 06: the SMD's archive is described as its "winning move" — the asset that decides the Ravenscroft endgame.
- 31: no RC beat stages the archive's deployment; the conflict engine's endgame RCs don't reference it.
**Why it is a problem:** A Chekhov's gun with no second act. The bible promises the reader an archive deployment and never delivers it.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Stage it: add an RC beat where the SMD deploys the archive (specify what the archive *does* — evidence, leverage, or a weapon).
2. Downgrade the language: the archive is *believed* to be a winning move (in-world reputation), and its non-deployment is the point.
3. Move the winning move to a different asset that 31 already stages.

### FAC-010 — HIGH — The Choir's Drowndust stockpile has no stated source or size
**Merges:** FAC-018 (Major)
**Location:** `12_FACTIONS.md` (REL-001) · `04_POWER_SYSTEM.md` (Drowndust doctrine)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 12: the Choir holds a Drowndust stockpile (implied by its deterrence posture).
- 04: "the Drowndust ban (prohibited in principle in 1949, made mandatory by the 1977 revision, re-ratified by the 1979 Second Protocols)" — with no licit production; no file states where a stockpile could come from or how large it is.
**Why it is a problem:** A banned substance's stockpile needs a provenance — pre-1977 caches, Gray Market synthesis, or Choir manufacture — and a size, because the stockpile's size determines whether the Choir is a nuisance or a strategic threat.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Source it: pre-1977 military caches (the Saratov precedent exists — MIL-006) plus slow Gray Market accumulation; state a size band.
2. Make the stockpile a bluff: the Choir *claims* a stockpile (deterrence by rumor), and the uncertainty is the plot.
3. Give the Choir a manufacturing method (a CANON change — the ban would need an exception; expensive).

### GEO-001 — HIGH — The ten-zone summary doesn't describe the actual registry
**Merges:** GEO-001 (High)
**Location:** `20_GEOGRAPHY.md` (ten-zone summary vs ZONE-001..010 registry)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 20: the ten-zone summary's prose (e.g. a "warded archive fault") doesn't match the ZONE-001..010 registry entries' contents (e.g. the same zone filed as a "scarred 1977 district").
**Why it is a problem:** Two descriptions of the same ten zones disagree. Every reference to a zone by its summary character is unreliable.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Rewrite the summary to describe the registry (mechanical).
2. Rewrite the registry to match the summary (heavier — the registry entries are referenced elsewhere).
3. Delete the summary and let the registry stand alone.

### HIST-001 — HIGH — EVENT-092's relocation residue: a Jakarta-primary cluster surviving in five places
**Merges:** PROTAG-003 (Moderate) · HIST-001 (High) · HIST-004 (Moderate) · HIST-007 (Moderate) · SOC-003 (Moderate)
**Location:** `25_TIMEmessaging app.md` (EVENT-092) · `24_HISTORY.md` (EVENT-092 entries) · `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` (Kota Tua night-market beat) · `17_MEDIA_SOCIETY.md` (EVENT-092 media entries)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 25 (EVENT-092): "…the **2022 Jakarta pressure-release** …" — the event's location, actors, and consequences are still staged in Jakarta-primary terms.
- 24: EVENT-092's history entries describe Jakarta institutions as the primary responders.
- 29: Arthur's formative Kota Tua night-market beat is staged as his *Ravenscroft* backstory anchor — the worker's PROTAG-003: the beat's geography is Jakarta's, not Ravenscroft's.
- 17: EVENT-092's media entries cite Jakarta outlets as the covering press.
**Why it is a problem:** EVENT-092 is the novel's inciting historical event and it is still half-Jakartan. The relocation's single largest residue cluster: five findings across four files, all pointing at the same half-moved event.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Complete the relocation: re-stage EVENT-092 as a Ravenscroft event with Jakarta as the secondary theater (mirror the 06/24 pattern — Albion primary, Indonesia secondary).
2. Keep EVENT-092 in Jakarta deliberately: it becomes the *reason* Arthur leaves for Ravenscroft (the Jakarta event is his push factor) — but then 24/29/17 must agree it was Jakarta.
3. Split EVENT-092 into two linked events (Jakarta release + Ravenscroft echo), which the pressure-wave physics supports.

### HIST-002 — HIGH — The 35/DATABASE index is stale in at least four places
**Merges:** HIST-002 (High) · NAR-005 (Moderate) · RED-017 (High)
**Location:** `35_CANON_DATABASE.md` · `27_SERIALIZATION_GUIDE.md` §27.2 · `31_CONFLICT_ENGINE.md` (CG-044, the ninth site)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 35: "ANOMALY-008 | C-B/R/Urban | An object that 'keeps what is placed in it'" — contradicts 28's C-C/R+P Erosion-transfer Rule (see POWER-005).
- 35: "SUP-004 | The Red Ledger | Gray-market auctioneers" — contradicts 12's PMC profile (see FAC-001).
- 27: the 35 row for CG-044 carries the pre-Jakarta label (see NAR-004).
- 31: the ninth site exists in the plot but the 35 L5 registry doesn't distinguish plot threads from mysteries (see NAR-005's original finding: "the ninth site is a plot thread, not an L5").
**Why it is a problem:** The database is the bible's fast-lookup layer; four stale rows mean every drafter who trusts 35 inherits the contradictions. (The parent's merge note is accepted: HIST-001's relocation rows and this index issue overlap — this issue owns the *index* rows, HIST-001 owns the *event* staging.)
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Rebuild the 35 rows from the patched canon files after all other fixes land (35 is last in the patch order).
2. Add a staleness protocol: 35 rows carry a source-file revision stamp, and any row older than its source is flagged.
3. Delete the duplicated rows and make 35 a pure index of pointers (no descriptive content to go stale).

### HIST-003 — HIGH — COUNTRY-001 is mislabeled and undersized for a Albion-primary bible
**Merges:** HIST-003 (High)
**Location:** `23_COUNTRIES.md` (COUNTRY-001)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 23: "| COUNTRY-001 | United States | Great power; BTA (GOV-001); largest Compact debtor, uses arrears as policy |" — while the relocation directive states "**Primary narrative region: EAST ASIA / PACIFIC RIM. Primary country: JAPAN (COUNTRY-001)**" and 35 lists "| COUNTRY-005 | Albion | **Primary country**; Special Measures Division (GOV-006); Ravenscroft City (CITY-033); Fukushima Rule origin |."
**Why it is a problem:** The country file for the *primary* setting country is mislabeled — the bible's geographic authority file misidentifies its own main stage.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Relabel COUNTRY-001 as Albion and write the Albion country profile the primary setting requires.
2. Keep COUNTRY-001 as Indonesia and create the Albion profile as COUNTRY-000 or a new primary entry — but the numbering then lies about primacy.
3. Merge the country profiles into the geography file and delist 23's numbering.

### SOC-001 — HIGH — Arthur's SSW visa does not legally cover his records-clerk work
**Merges:** SOC-001 (Major)
**Location:** `17_MEDIA_SOCIETY.md` (SSW program, Arthur's employment) · `25_TIMEmessaging app.md` (EVENT-087)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 17: Arthur works as an insurance records clerk — but his SSW (Specified Skilled Worker) visa category does not legally cover that work.
- 25 (EVENT-087): "…a flagged applicant was hired — **PROVISIONAL**, approver redacted…" / the worker's finding: "someone wanted him inside."
**Why it is a problem:** The protagonist's legal right to be in Albion — and at his specific desk — doesn't hold. Either the bible fixes the paperwork or the story's premise (a documented foreign worker under Quiet institutional watch) collapses the first time anyone checks.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Make the mismatch deliberate: NQA's Quiet hiring apparatus bent the rules — *someone wanted him inside* — and the irregular paperwork becomes his precarity engine (the Tuesday problem's sharpest edge: he can't afford scrutiny). See decision (d).
2. Change the visa category to one that covers records-clerk work (but the SSW program's constraints are load-bearing for the precariat theme — check before changing).
3. Change the job to SSW-covered work (but the records clerk *placement* is Threshold-adjacent by design — cf. INFO-001 option 1).

### NAR-001 — HIGH — Arthur "never met another Loud adult" contradicts the novel's own backstory
**Merges:** FAC-010 (Minor) · NAR-003 (Moderate) · RED-011 (Moderate)
**Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §A.4 ("…he has never met another Loud adult…") · `32_NARRATIVE_THREADS.md` §32.3 · `12_FACTIONS.md` (FAC-010's original context)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 29: Arthur "has never met another Loud adult" — the worker's RED-011/NAR-003 finding: this contradicts the backstory's own staged encounters (the timeline's Jakarta events put Loud adults in his vicinity; the Tuesday problem's whole premise is that Quiet institutions have been *watching* him).
- 32: the Loud-pipeline plot requires Loud adults to be recruitable quantities — a world where the protagonist never met one strains the pipeline's plausibility.
**Why it is a problem:** The line is load-bearing for Arthur's isolation, but the world keeps putting Loud adults in his path. Either the line or the encounters must give.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Narrow the line: he never *knowingly* met another Loud adult — the encounters happened, he didn't recognize them (pairs with the Veil Reflex and his late Awakening).
2. Keep the line absolute and remove the staged encounters from his vicinity (timeline surgery — expensive).
3. Make the line in-world belief: it's what Arthur *believes*, and the novel reveals he was wrong (a reveal beat).

### NAR-002 — HIGH — Arthur's KNF/SMD file custody chain contradicts itself
**Merges:** NAR-004 (Moderate) · RED-012 (Moderate)
**Location:** `32_NARRATIVE_THREADS.md` §32.5 · `06_GOVERNMENTS.md` (SMD) · `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md`
**Conflicting statements:**
- 32: the custody chain for Arthur's KNF file is stated one way in the handoff beat and another way in the SMD's archive description.
- 06: the SMD's Ravenscroft field office "borrows the prefectural quiet desk's Lantern examiners and never returns them on time" — the file-priority fight implies the SMD holds the file; the handoff beat implies the KNF never released it.
**Why it is a problem:** The file is the MacGuffin of the first act's institutional plot. Its location must be single-valued at every beat.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Fix the chain: KNF holds the original, SMD holds a *copy* obtained through the liaison fight — the copy/original distinction becomes a plot point (which one the Quiet Desk steals?).
2. Give the file a single custodian and rewrite the contradicting beat.
3. Make the duplication load-bearing: there are *two* files (the KNF's and the SMD's), and their disagreement is the mystery.

### SER-001 — HIGH — Uses 13+ have no authorized generative principle
**Merges:** SER-002 (Moderate) · RED-018 (High)
**Location:** `27_SERIALIZATION_GUIDE.md` §27.5 · `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B (the twelve uses)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 27: "Uses 13+ are authorized" — but no principle authorizes them: the twelve uses are "the documented ladder," and nothing says what makes a thirteenth use *lawful* within the world's physics.
- 29: the discovery grammar (observe → hypothesize → test → pay) is described as Arthur's *method*, not as a physical law.
**Why it is a problem:** The serialization plan promises post-ladder invention, but the world has no rule for what counts as a legal new use. Without one, every Use 13+ is authorial fiat — the exact "secretly omnipotent" failure mode the protagonist spec forbids.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Promote the discovery grammar to a physical principle: the twelve are the *documented* ladder; new uses are generated only by observe→hypothesize→test→pay, and force/knowledge-without-interpretation never generates (conservative — no new mechanics).
2. Tie generation to the ratchet: new lag-depths unlock new observable phenomena, which the grammar converts to uses (pairs with the ratchet decision (k)).
3. Cap the ladder: there are no Uses 13+ — the twelve are complete, and post-ladder growth is *combinations*, not inventions (most restrictive, cleanest).

### REL-001 — HIGH — The Quiet Garden's founding date and census don't reconcile
**Merges:** FAC-005 (Major) · REL-002 (High) · HIST-005 (Moderate)
**Location:** `12_FACTIONS.md` (REL-004) · `21_RELIGION.md` (Quiet Garden) · `24_HISTORY.md` (1911 vs 1962)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 12: "Founded (in its modern form) in Kyoto in 1911; now ~2,000 professed practitioners in houses on four continents, plus ~20,000 lay affiliates."
- 21: "**Members:** ~8,000 professed (monastics); ~60,000 lay affiliates."
- 24: the founding is dated 1911 in one entry and 1962 in another — a 51-year gap.
**Why it is a problem:** A major religious institution's founding is triple-dated and its size is double-stated. The 1911/1962 gap is 51 years — not a rounding error.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Two-stage history: 1911 lineage founding, 1962 refounding/recharter (the pattern matches the SMD's own 1995 recharter narrative) — both dates survive with distinct meanings.
2. Pick one date and rewrite the other file's entry.
3. Make the dispute in-world: the Garden claims 1911 (lineage), historians say 1962 (institution) — the disagreement is canonical and characterizes both sides.

### REL-002 — HIGH — A dozen Attuned in holy orders breaks the Compact's own rule
**Merges:** REL-001 (High) · FAC-006 (Major)
**Location:** `21_RELIGION.md` · `12_FACTIONS.md` · `02_WORLD_RULES.md` (Attuned-clergy restrictions)
**Conflicting statements:**
- 21: "a dozen Attuned in holy orders" are described across the faiths.
- 02/12: the Compact's posture toward Attuned clergy is restrictive (the census, the precariat gap, the Quiet Desk's interest in Loud clergy).
**Why it is a problem:** Twelve is not a handful and not a secret — it's a visible cohort the Compact's rules should have processed. Either the rule or the cohort must give.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Make it a secret breach: the twelve exist *in violation* of the Compact's posture, and the Quiet Desk's file on them is a plot thread.
2. Remove the Attuned clergy: rewrite the twelve as Loud (non-Attuned) clergy with Threshold sensitivity — keeps the religious texture, removes the rule break.
3. Charter them: a named Compact exemption for chaplaincy roles (medical/hospice), inspected and revocable — pairs with FAC-002's charter option.

### REL-003 — HIGH — The Drowned Choir's founding is double-dated (1953 vs 1965)
**Merges:** FAC-007 (Major) · REL-005 (High)
**Location:** `12_FACTIONS.md` (REL-001) · `21_RELIGION.md` · `24_HISTORY.md`
**Conflicting statements:**
- 21: "Origin: founded 1965 in Marseille by Miriam Adler (CHAR-008), a hospice chaplain." / "the Choir dates its founding to 1965."
- 24: the founding appears as 1953 (the circle) in another entry — a 12-year gap.
**Why it is a problem:** The primary antagonist faction's origin is double-dated. The 1953/1965 window also overlaps the Seismograph construction (1963–68) — the wrong date changes what the Choir predates.
**Proposed solutions:**
1. Two-stage origin: 1953 (the drowning event / first congregation), 1965 (the Choir's formal organization) — both dates survive.
2. Pick one date; the other becomes an in-world historiographical dispute.
3. Tie the founding to a dated event (EVENT-028's mass-Awakening pile or the 1963–68 Seismograph build) to anchor it.
