Q-001 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't the Attuned just channel through proxies to dodge Erosion? → Erosion prices the *channeler*; 30's healer's paradox: aggressive Bone healing transfers Erosion-risk to the healer. There is no proxy — only a new payer.
Q-002 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't hospitals keep Echoes of dead specialists as consultants? → Echoes degrade and are not the person (02/I); 26.2's I-flag handling destroys originals after summarization. A degrading echo is a liability, not staff.
Q-003 [ANSWERED — 14] Why isn't borrowed-matter crime (a knife that reverts in 72h) the perfect-murder industry? → 14 names it explicitly: "the weapon reverts, the evidence reverts, and the law can't quite grip the 72 hours in between" — a legal gray zone the Compact failed to close three times. The constraint is the conjurer: you need a rare Attuned willing to burn Erosion for your murder, and unlicensed conjuration is inspected (02/II).
Q-004 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't Tide-mark readers settle every historical dispute? → Tide-marks are impressions, never editable and never video (02/I); reading them is T2 Lantern work costing Erosion (30); interpretation is a skill (29 B.11 Use 4).
Q-005 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't interrogators use suggestion seeding routinely? → Resistible by awareness/identity/training/stubbornness, always produces backlash, per-link unreliability compounds geometrically (02/I); 30.4 Scenario 2 works the counter.
Q-006 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why doesn't anyone steal a useful Resonance? → Requires killing the source in a Drowning-adjacent window, needs a T3+ Lantern examiner, is Seismograph-visible as T4+, needs Tribunal warrant or it's double-murder, the thief inherits the victim's Fathoms at 2× burn (02/I; 04). Three corpses in vaults prove why.
Q-007 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't couriers Slip contraband past every checkpoint? → Anchors require recognition by another living person; warded zones shred arrivals; hardened wards shred all non-whitelisted; each Slip costs Erosion; relay chains are "economically dead" within three hops (02/I).
Q-008 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't the rich buy Erosion reversal? → "Erosion is irreversible. Nothing reverses it" (02/I). Vesper's bet is STATUS: THEORY (33 L3) — the most expensive bet, not a product.
Q-009 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't Attuned build causal-bubble fortresses? → Bubbles are line-of-sight scale, minutes-long, ruinously expensive (02/I); anomaly Rules can exceed caps but inherit proportional costs (visibility, pressure, attention).
Q-010 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why isn't there a commodity market in anomalies? → Non-fungible by CANON-LOCKED rule (02/II): auctions of specific items with specific Rules only. "You cannot scale what you cannot reproduce."
Q-011 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why doesn't anyone get rich conjuring gold? → Borrowed wealth reverts (02/II); financial precognition is Lantern-screened at every major exchange, and "lucky traders keep Drowning."
Q-012 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why doesn't licensed conjuration break the masquerade (conjured scaffolding on visible sites)? → It's licensed, regulated like explosives handling, and "the economics usually favor mundane alternatives" — visible but boring, a premium product for sites cranes can't reach (02/II).
Q-013 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why is information scarce when Seeps are everywhere? → "Raw power is common enough to be cheap; reliable, specific, actionable knowledge about Rules, Tide-marks, and faction intentions is what money actually buys" (02/II). Rules are non-fungible; every Seep is unprecedented in specifics (27).
Q-014 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why can't the Veil be trained away by exposure therapy? → It's an involuntary cognitive immune response; removal requires birth or a Seep event (02/III; 33 item 7).
Q-015 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why does any footage survive at all if rot is proportional? → Proportionality is the answer: "A T1's party trick films fine. A T5 event leaves melted sensors" (02/III). Weak Seeps are filmable; strong ones aren't.
Q-016 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why don't factions just show each other proof? → The deepfake dividend cuts both ways (02/III): synthetic media destroyed video trust for everyone, so "the Compact's closed verification protocols (witnessed, multi-sensor, in-person) are the only currency of proof."
Q-017 [ANSWERED — 02/IV] Why would a smart faction leave Arthur alone if he's valuable? → 02/IV: "Competence is distributed" — every "why didn't they just…?" must have an answer in incentives/constraints/cost/law/politics/Erosion. The audit's Enemy simulation (§5 of NARRATIVE_AUDIT) finds one for each tier.
Q-018 [ANSWERED — 02/IV] Why doesn't the novel just reveal the Laggard's nature in chapter 10? → 02/IV: "Mystery is budgeted"; 33: L5-2's resolution condition requires Arthur to earn the test himself.
Q-019 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't the Drowned leave wills via Tide-marks? → Tide-marks are impressions of the past, viewable never editable (02/I) — and Drowning dissolves the pattern; there's no one left to impress.
Q-020 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't ward-makers just ward the whole planet? → Wards are architectural, expensive (04: "the reason secret facilities are built like temples"); wards *dampen* but the High Tide raises pressure everywhere. Cost and scale.
Q-021 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why doesn't the Seismograph network see EVERYTHING (it's global)? → It sees *large* Tide events (~T3+, unregistered Slipping, active T4+). T0–T2 and quiet T1s are below threshold — which is why the claims-ML-clerk-Lantern stack exists (27).
Q-022 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't time-dilation bubbles enable infinite preparation? → Minutes-long, line-of-sight, ruinous Erosion (02/I). "There are no time-loop fortresses."
Q-023 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why doesn't anyone Slip into the past via Tide-marks? → Tide-marks are viewable, never editable; the past cannot be changed (02/I). Viewing isn't travel.
Q-024 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't the Attuned pool Erosion (one person pays for all)? → Erosion is per-pattern wear (02/I); the healer's paradox moves risk, never deletes it (30.1).
Q-025 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why don't insurers just exclude everything supernatural? → Then nobody buys Quiet policies and the masquerade loses a load-bearing wall (26.2 pillar 6: "A victim with an open claim is a victim who isn't posting about it"). Exclusion would be *worse* for secrecy.
Q-026 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the Veil protect the Loud from trauma? → The Veil *edits* Seep memories in the Quiet; the Loud are defined by the Veil barely touching them (36). No edit, no protection — which is why Loud support groups exist (IND-002).
Q-027 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why don't T5s just do one big channeling and retire? → One big channeling *is* retirement: 25–60 Fathoms per serious use, "rarely past 50" alive (30.2). The arithmetic is the plot.
Q-028 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't echoes vote, own property, or testify? → They are not the person and they degrade (02/I); 14's courts would face depth-adjusted chaos. (CG-030 "The Echo's Pension" is the conflict this question generates.)
Q-029 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why doesn't the Ledger just print soundings? → Soundings are ledger entries for netting obligations about unique things, not circulating currency (09/36). "Printing" them would be forging settlement records inside its own member banks — fraud against itself.
Q-030 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't the Hollow Men just dive to the Undertow and look at the thing behind the Laggard? → They "dive" (SUP-006); Crane's warning (EVENT-094) suggests one already noticed it noticing. Looking is not understanding — and 33 L5-2's resolution condition requires *Arthur's* earned test, not a diver's field trip.
Q-031 [ANSWERED — 02/IV] Why is "power is legible" not violated by the smiling mirror reflection? → See RED-002; the reflection is the one phenomenon whose legibility is currently undeposited.
Q-032 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why can't a T6 just ward-break into anywhere? → T4+ "breach wards" is in-tier (30.1), but every T3+ channeling is Seismograph-visible globally within minutes (02/II) and costs Erosion at 40+/use (30.2). They *can*; they can't afford to, twice.
Q-033 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why don't smugglers Slip Drowndust instead of shipping it? → Slip-courier chains are "economically dead" within three hops (02/I); each link pays full Erosion. The straits (RC-004/038) exist because physics prices teleportation out of the freight market.
Q-034 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't static rot destroy the Laggard's shadow itself? → The shadow is Membrane-darkness, not Undertow-structured information (29 B.11 Use 10) — rot eats Undertow-structured recordings, not darkness.
Q-035 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't the Attuned live forever via pattern-capture? → "Patterns can only be captured during collapse" (02/I) — capture is theft-murder, not backup; and "no true resurrection" means the copy is an echo, not continuation.
Q-036 [ANSWERED — 02/IV] Why doesn't the Compact just recruit every Loud child? → 26.1 L2: the Compact's unwritten position is "the Loud are a self-maintaining sensor network we don't have to pay for." Recruitment would cost money and create files; the current arrangement is free.
Q-037 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why don't Tide-brokers corner the market on Rules? → Rules are non-fungible and information is the scarce resource (02/II); you can broker *a* Rule's sale (Red Ledger does) but not the *market* — there's no index to corner.
Q-038 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't the dead just… stay dead quietly (echo hauntings)? → Echoes degrade (02/I); hauntings are Organism/Event Seeps with Rules, not tenants (28/30).
Q-039 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the Veil edit the *Loud* over decades (slow erosion of memory)? → CANON: "The Veil Reflex barely touches him" (01/29) — barely isn't zero, but Loud recall is defined as permanent (26.1 L2). The immunity is the trait.
Q-040 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't two Attuned just trade Fathoms (I'll burn for you)? → Fathoms aren't transferable; channeling wears the *channeler's* pattern (02/I). The healer's paradox is the closest mechanism and it *adds* risk (30.1).
Q-041 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why doesn't anyone arbitrage the 72-hour reversion window (borrowed collateral)? → 09/02: the Ledger settles in soundings against *unique* collateral; borrowed matter's reversion is a known, priced risk — and "borrowed wealth reverts" (02/II) is the first thing every compliance desk screens.
Q-042 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't the Attuned just wear Anchor restraints in reverse (amplify)? → Anchors disrupt the Hollow Pattern (36); disruption isn't tunable amplification — it's jamming (04).
Q-043 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the deepfake dividend destroy the Compact's internal proof protocols? → It did for *video*; that's why the protocols are "witnessed, multi-sensor, in-person" (02/III; 26.2). The dividend forced the protocols offline, not into uselessness.
Q-044 [ANSWERED — 02/IV] Why doesn't "competence is distributed" make Arthur irrelevant? → Because his assets (exceptional recall, claims position, tether) are *positional*, not competence-based (NARRATIVE_AUDIT §4). The world doesn't need him to be the smartest — it needs him to be where the paper flows.
Q-045 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't the Quiet just evolve past the Veil (selection pressure)? → The Veil is a cognitive immune response, not a single-gene trait under selection (02/III; 33 item 7); Loudness is "birth or a Seep event — both rare."
Q-046 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why doesn't the Compact tax Gray Markets instead of fighting them? → It partly does — the Ledger, soundings, Argent Vault (09/36). Full taxation would require jurisdiction over zones designed to lack it (Gray Markets, 36).
Q-047 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't causal bubbles enable FTL messaging (stretch seconds into hours)? → Line-of-sight scale, minutes max, ruinous Erosion (02/I) — and anomaly-Rule bubbles inherit proportional costs (visibility, pressure). No free bandwidth.
Q-048 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the Veil edit digital records (it edits minds)? → It doesn't need to: static rot eats Undertow-structured recordings (02/III), and mundane records of *claims* are the insurers' business (that's Arthur's job — the paper survives because it's paperwork about the aftermath, not the event).
Q-049 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't Drowned Attuned leave their Fathoms as a resource? → Drowning is pattern dissolution "usually leaving a new Seep behind" (36) — the residue is a hazard, not a battery (02/I: Erosion irreversible; nothing harvestable).
Q-050 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why don't exchanges just ban all Attuned employees? → "Lucky traders keep Drowning" (02/II) — they do screen (Lantern-screened compliance); small-scale precognition is the residue they can't fully filter, which is why the screening exists.
Q-051 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why doesn't anyone weaponize the Veil itself (mass forgetting as a weapon)? → The Veil is involuntary and universal (02/III); it can't be aimed. Suggestion seeding is the aimable cousin and it's resistible with backlash (02/I).
Q-052 [ANSWERED — 02/IV] Why doesn't the world just… tell everyone (controlled disclosure)? → 26.4's six reasons + mutually assured disclosure: disclosure is symmetric (every discloser is implicated), the audience forgets, and the minority report says the failure mode is arithmetic, not revelation. Controlled disclosure was tried in pieces (Lagos 2023, EVENT-089) — 4,000 remembered for a week, then containment.
Q-053 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't the Attuned just Slip out of warded cells? → "Hardened wards shred all non-whitelisted arrivals — recognized or not" (02/I). The cell checks the whitelist, not the memory.
Q-054 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why doesn't the Tally just replace the Ledger? → Different trust domains: the Tally is hawala-style trust clearing outside the Ledger (36); the Ledger is institutional settlement (09). Gray vs. white need both.
Q-055 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't noise farming accidentally create tulpas (belief makes Seeps)? → Tulpa theory is STATUS: IN-WORLD MISCONCEPTION (36); the 2020 attention-dip is "the tulpa grain-of-truth, weaponized as theory" (25 EVENT-081) — attention *modulates* pressure, mechanism UNKNOWN (33 L5-4). Farming doesn't create; at most it modulates.
Q-056 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why don't the Attuned just channel at T1 forever (free power)? → Many do — "the hidden world's plankton" (30.1), 90,000 T2 professionals, decades-long careers (30.2). That's the *normal* case; the novels are about the abnormal ones.
Q-057 [ANSWERED — 02/IV] Why doesn't the Undertow just… stop (if it doesn't want anything)? → It doesn't want anything *including* stopping (01.VII; 33 L5-3). Pressure isn't volition.
Q-058 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why don't the corporations just buy the Compact? → The Compact has no army and no balance sheet to buy (07: "no army"); it's a treaty secretariat. You can't acquire a coordination protocol — and 08's 8.7 anti-billionaire mechanisms are "near-airtight" per the final audit.
Q-059 [ANSWERED — 02/I] Why doesn't resurrection-via-echo count as breaking "no true resurrection"? → The rule *defines* the boundary: echoes "have memories up to the point of pattern-capture, but are not the person. Echoes degrade" (02/I). The returned thing isn't the deceased — that's the horror, not the loophole.
Q-060 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the Veil protect against *reading* about Seeps (text)? → 17: "text survives static rot better than video" — written accounts are "the leak vector the professionals actually worry about," which is why the shelving protocol prioritizes flooding *text* search. The Veil edits *exposure* memory; reading is handled by noise, not neurology.

## B. THE LAGGARD (Q-061–Q-120)

Q-061 [ANSWERED — 29/B.5] Why doesn't Arthur just live in a warded room to hide the light-drinking? → Wards *dampen* Membrane-side effects but cannot sever the tether (29/B.5) — and "deep use inside wards remains possible; it is simply louder at the entity's end." Warding is a dimmer, not a cloak, and it makes the *other* end notice faster.
Q-062 [ANSWERED — 29/B.5] Why doesn't he just stay out of-phase permanently (Use 5)? → 3 seconds per step, no interaction, can't pass solid matter, Lantern-sensitives feel the wrongness, and the entity notices (29/B.5, B.11). Three seconds of ghosthood, not a lifestyle.
Q-063 [ANSWERED — 29/B.6] Why doesn't he deep-lag for 3 hours every week (Use 6)? → A 3-minute deepening costs a day in bed; Ilsa's 3-hour maximum cost a week and "it almost didn't give her back"; the ratchet bills permanently; "at 3 hours, he was the one being observed" (29/B.6, B.11).
Q-064 [ANSWERED — 29/B.11] Why doesn't he store weapons in the shadow (Use 7)? → "Only what the shadow can cover; nothing living; retrieve within the lag window or it is gone" (29/B.11) — a coin-sized hold with total-loss risk and a permanent ratchet charge per use. It's a pocket, not an armory.
Q-065 [ANSWERED — 29/B.11] Why doesn't the clean recording (Use 10) end the masquerade on day one? → He hasn't discovered it yet (discovery ~2024, accidental); when he does, 26.4's six anti-Snowden reasons still apply to *broadcast* — the escrow works because it's *targeted* disclosure to parties with institutional memory (29/B.11). See also RED-008.
Q-066 [ANSWERED — 29/B.8] Why doesn't a faction just kill him and take the tether from the corpse? → Transfer goes to the nearest *Loud witness*; staged/assisted/arranged deaths *dissipate* the tether — "no dormancy, no fallback holder" (29/B.8). Murder is denial, not acquisition — and the 1993 Surabaya attempt proved it.
Q-067 [ANSWERED — 29/B.8] Why doesn't a faction just kill him with NO witnesses to deny rivals the tether? → They could — that's denial, and nobody wants denial; they want the *Rule* (EVENT-093's buyer wants to buy, not burn). Plus murdering a filed anomaly-holder invites the seizure-doctrine ladder and Tribunal referral (26.2).
Q-068 [ANSWERED — 29/B.8] Why doesn't the transfer just… pick the strongest nearby Loud? → "Proximity + Loudness, not inheritance, not worthiness, not fate" (29/B.8). The universe doesn't choose holders. That's load-bearing theme, not a bug.
Q-069 [ANSWERED — 29/B.2] Why don't photographers' meters lying near him expose him? → "Difficulty being photographed well (meters lie)" is chronic (29/B.7) — but a bad photo is a *bad photo*, not evidence. The deepfake dividend (02/III) eats the rest.
Q-070 [ANSWERED — 29/B.2] Why don't the Lantern-headaches within 10m get him flagged? → Mild headaches near *a* clerk are noise; Lantern-sensitives aren't a dragnet (29/B.2). The 2020 Vesper scare (B.10 #5) shows what happens when someone *does* follow up: Fathom scan 0, file closed.
Q-071 [ANSWERED — 29/B.10] Why doesn't the BPF just reopen the Attuned file on him? → "It was closed for lack of a Hollow Pattern" (29/B.10 #2). Reopening needs new evidence; the sensor misread once. The jeopardy is that it *could* reopen — that's the thread.
Q-072 [ANSWERED — 29/B.3] Why doesn't Arthur just read Ilsa's journals on day one? → He doesn't have them — they're held by the Cartographers' Exchange; Pram arrives with them 2024-09-18 (EVENT-100). See RED-007 for the 28-year delay.
Q-073 [ANSWERED — 29/B.11] Why doesn't Use 3 (seeding anchor) make him immune to all manipulation? → "It anchors *actions*, not motives; a clever Choir operator works with that" (29/B.11). The counter is in the bible.
Q-074 [ANSWERED — 29/B.11] Why doesn't Use 4 (Tide-mark dowsing) make him a historian-for-hire? → "Tide-marks are impressions, not video; interpretation is a skill he must learn from the journals" (29/B.11). The tool needs training he doesn't have yet.
Q-075 [ANSWERED — 29/B.11] Why doesn't Use 9 (unobserved observation) make him the ultimate spy? → "At deep lag you are not the only observer" (29/B.11) — Ilsa's margin warning. The entity watches back.
Q-076 [ANSWERED — 29/B.6] Why doesn't the ratchet just… stop if he never uses it extremely? → Ordinary life: no permanent change (29/B.6). 3.0→3.1s over ~7 years is baseline drift. The clock only runs on extreme use — which the plot will demand.
Q-077 [ANSWERED — 29/B.7] Why don't the dreams drive him insane (dark water, something standing with him)? → All three holders reported them; none described the something's shape — "It has a *direction*" (29/B.7). Dread, not damage — so far. Whether attention is dangerous is UNKNOWN (load-bearing, 33 L5-2 adjacent).
Q-078 [ANSWERED — 29/B.2] Why don't dogs/cats/children constitute a detection network? → They react (dogs worst, cats curious, children point — 29/B.2) but can't file reports. Street-level texture, not a sensor grid.
Q-079 [ANSWERED — 29/B.5] Why can't he just give the shadow away (Ilsa tried 3 times)? → "It waited. I came back from the ferry and it was standing where I stand" (29/B.2). Divestiture is impossible; that's canon.
Q-080 [ANSWERED — 29/B.11] Why doesn't the flare (Use 12) get used as a routine distress signal? → "A flare he can't unfire… everyone comes. Everyone" (29/B.11). See RED-020 (SER-005 overlap): the first flare burns the T1 filing permanently.
Q-081 [ANSWERED — 29/B.12] Why doesn't the Meridian Vector get a "window" tier after the Laggard? → "The classification system has no tier for windows, because windows don't channel" (29/B.12) — and there's exactly *one* known window. Bureaucracies don't add tiers for n=1.
Q-082 [PATCH REQUIRED → RED-001] What is the detection radius of a 3-second Use-5 step vs. a deepening vs. the flare? → 29/B.5 says deep use is visible "across kilometers" to Lantern-sensitives and instruments, and Use 5's counter says sensitives "feel the wrongness" — but no per-tier radius is given. If every 3-second dodge pings kilometers, the SMD's instruments have a log of Arthur's Kota Tua accident (EVENT-092) the thin file doesn't mention.
Q-083 [ANSWERED — 29/B.3] Why is the lag 3 seconds and not, say, 3 minutes at baseline? → Translation delay between substrates (33 item 6: "author-level CANON"); the Undertow has no time, the Membrane renders position late. The number is the mechanism's signature.
Q-084 [ANSWERED — 29/B.6] Why does fatigue lengthen the lag (2.8s→8s) without ratcheting? → Temporary deepening vs. permanent ratchet are distinct (29/B.3 vs B.6): exhaustion stretches the window; only *extreme use* adds permanent fractions. An 8s exhausted lag is a longer window, not a longer clock.
Q-085 [ANSWERED — 29/B.1] Why did three examinations (1992/2007/2021) all conclude T1? → "The vector measures *output*; the Laggard's output is near-zero" (29/B.1). The instruments are designed to be wrong about windows — structural irony, not examiner incompetence. (The "Reactive" dodge in older notes is flagged as in-world behavior, 29/B.1.)
Q-086 [PATCH REQUIRED → RED-002] Why does his mirror reflection sometimes smile when he isn't (29/B.2), and what prices that? → Documented by Ilsa, observed by Arthur twice — a phenomenon with no Rule, no cost, no thread registration. 02/IV demands legibility ("if Arthur cannot eventually figure out *why* something happened, it should not have happened"); 33's closed L5 set doesn't include it.
Q-087 [ANSWERED — 29/B.11] Why doesn't he photograph the shadow *now*, before Use 10's "discovery"? → He avoids cameras ("difficulty being photographed well," 29/B.7); photographers' meters lie (29/B.2); and Use 10's discovery is specifically a photo of the shadow *mid-replay* developed normally — the accident requires the replay, not just the shadow.
Q-088 [ANSWERED — 29/B.2] Why doesn't the voice-memo echo rot like other recordings? → Same principle as Use 10 (29/B.11): the shadow is Membrane-darkness, not Undertow-structured information — rot eats the latter (02/III). The echo is Membrane-side residue.
Q-089 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why haven't the Cartographers just *taken* him (they want to experiment)? → As of canon date they just reclassified him (EVENT-100, 2024-09-18) — the taking is *upcoming*, not overdue. 29/B.9: "Their interest is scientific, which makes it the most dangerous kind: they will *experiment*."
Q-090 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't Vesper just offer him a job (clinical, consensual)? → That's exactly their threat profile: "well-funded — and impossible to leave" (29/B.9). The novel hasn't reached the offer yet; Nwosu is still hunting (EVENT-091).
Q-091 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Choir just baptize him by force? → A street preacher tried recruiting him in 2022 (29/B.9) — failed. Force against a filed T1 curiosity in SMD jurisdiction buys a seizure-doctrine response (26.2) for zero gain (they'd canonize the tether, not extract it).
Q-092 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Pale Court just marry into the Reed line? → Vivienne Ashworth "asks after 'the Reed boy'" (EVENT-098) — the courtship (literal or otherwise) is the thread. 33 L3: Court eugenics is "predictively weak — inheritance raises odds, guarantees nothing," so they'd *court*, not seize.
Q-093 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Red Ledger just buy Arthur himself? → The Factor's inquiry is for the *Rule* (EVENT-093: "a buyer wants the Laggard"); buying the person doesn't transfer the tether (29/B.8 — engineered transfers dissipate). You can't close on the asset.
Q-094 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the KNF just repatriate him (he's Indonesian, ex-KNF's Aisha knows him)? → He's a legal SSW worker in Albion (29); the KNF is "out of jurisdiction now" (29/B.9); repatriation needs cause. Aisha's unfiled follow-up is the dog that hasn't barked (32/C).
Q-095 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Alon Combine just kidnap the clerk who remembers? → They price the *rumor* (29/B.9) — kidnapping a legal migrant worker in SMD jurisdiction for a rumor is bad economics. "The Alon Combine wants the analyst, not the analysis" (RC-004) — recruitment, not rendition.
Q-096 [PATCH REQUIRED → RED-003] How does the dead-man's-switch escrow actually work (who holds it, what triggers release, what stops seizure of the mechanism)? → 29/B.11: "copies… keyed to multiple faction and civilian recipients… released automatically if he fails to check in." No mechanism is specified — yet the escrow is his entire defense against disappearing. A switch that can't be seized, can't be spoofed, and triggers "automatically" is infrastructure doing heavy lifting off-page.
Q-097 [ANSWERED — 29/B.11] Why doesn't Use 11 (Erosion-free operation) make every operator trade places with him? → "His currencies are finite too — the ratchet only runs one way, and attention compounds" (29/B.11). They'd trade the *Erosion* bill, not the *attention* bill — and the Garden/others know the difference.
Q-098 [ANSWERED — 29/B.4] Why can't he just *command* the shadow (it's "his")? → "None — it is passive and always-on. There is no trigger, no command, no off switch" (29/B.4). "Use" means reading, deepening, or stepping in — all learned.
Q-099 [ANSWERED — 29/B.5] Why can't the shadow affect matter if it's "real"? → "It cannot affect matter directly. It is a shadow… everything it does, it does by *being read* or *being entered*" (29/B.5). The limitation is the point.
Q-100 [PATCH REQUIRED → RED-004] Does Use 10's rot-immunity hold for deep-lag replays (3-minute/3-hour windows showing high-pressure events)? → 29/B.11: "The shadow is Membrane-darkness, not Undertow-structured information — so recordings of the shadow do not rot." But a deep-lag replay of a T5 event would render Undertow-adjacent content — is the *recording* still Membrane-darkness at that depth? Unspecified; the most abusable edge of the most dangerous use.
Q-101 [ANSWERED — 29/B.6] Why doesn't social cost just… fade (people get used to dim rooms)? → "It is exhausting, and it is forever" (29/B.6) — the cost is permanent and ambient: dates end early, landlords ask questions. Habituation by others doesn't remove his performance of normalcy.
Q-102 [ANSWERED — 29/B.8] Why did the tether pick a *dockworker* (Yusuf) for 21 years without incident? → "Proof that an ordinary man can carry the tether without being consumed by it" (29/B.8) — the control group to Ilsa's experiment. Nothing happened *because* nothing was attempted; the tether is passive until read.
Q-103 [ANSWERED — 29/B.8] Why didn't Ilsa's 1993 Surabaya entry (staged-death attempt) warn *everyone* off engineering transfers? → It did — that's why the rule is in her journals and in 29/B.8. The 1993 would-be heir is the reason the rule is *known*.
Q-104 [PATCH REQUIRED → RED-009] The filing says "monitor biennially" (29/B.1); the last exam was 2021 — was there a 2023 exam, and what did it conclude? → Unmentioned anywhere. If it happened and yawned, the "protection expiring" timeline needs it; if austerity (RC-040) skipped it, that's a plot-relevant fact. One line missing.
Q-105 [ANSWERED — 29/B.10] Why do twelve misunderstandings persist if the truth is in Ilsa's journals? → The journals are in the Exchange; the misunderstandings are *folk/field* readings by people who never saw them (29/B.10). "All are believed by someone" — distribution, not logic, sustains them.
Q-106 [ANSWERED — 29/B.3] Why doesn't the cold halo (1–2°C) show on thermal cameras and start a rumor? → A 1–2°C halo on a thermal camera is a *cold spot*, not a person-shaped anomaly — and "on bad days" only (29/B.7). Thermal noise eats it; the deepfake dividend eats the rest.
Q-107 [ANSWERED — 29/B.12] Why doesn't Arthur's weakness get him killed in chapter 3? → "The Laggard's weakness is camouflage. Being filed T1 is the best ward Arthur owns" (30.4 Scenario 5) + 26.3: he's the cheapest sensor and least protected — factions' first instinct is to *acquire*, not kill (dead assets file no claims).
Q-108 [PATCH REQUIRED → RED-010] The "Membrane" terminology note (29-local, PROVISIONAL): "Drafter A may rename it in assembly" — is this canon or a drafting Post-it? → A process instruction embedded in the protagonist's canonical file. Either the term is canon (drop the note) or it's provisional (don't ship it in v1.2).
Q-109 [ANSWERED — 29/B.11] Why is the discovery order fixed (1→12)? → "The order is the discovery order — the novel's progression spine" (29/B.11): each use's *observation* unlocks the next (the mirror delay teaches lag-as-recording; the Kota Tua accident teaches stepping-in). It's pedagogical, not mechanical.
Q-110 [ANSWERED — 29/B.6] Why doesn't the entity just talk to him (it notices deep use)? → Whether it's intelligent is UNKNOWN (33 L5-2, author-level). "Anticipation isn't intelligence; a crystal 'anticipates' geometry." Talking would resolve L5-2 for free.
Q-111 [ANSWERED — 29/B.5] Why can't wards *sever* the tether if they suppress everything else? → "The tether is not a Membrane phenomenon to begin with" (29/B.5). Wards are architectural Membrane-suppression; the tether's other end isn't in the Membrane.
Q-112 [ANSWERED — 29/B.8] What happens if two Loud witnesses are equidistant at the holder's death? → Unspecified — but "nearest" with a tie is a measure-zero edge; the journals' rule is proximity+Loudness, and Ilsa's 1993 entry covers *engineering*, not ties. (Fair to leave; not load-bearing.)
Q-113 [ANSWERED — 29/B.7] Why does brackish-water smell follow deep use ("bau payau")? → Yusuf's logbook and Arthur both report it (29/B.7) — sensory residue of Undertow contact, consistent with the dreams of dark water. Texture with a mechanism (tether contact), not decoration.
Q-114 [ANSWERED — 29/B.2] Why do Lantern-sensitives get headaches within ~10m? → The tether drinks ambient information-pressure (29/B.2, B.5); sensitives feel the local deficit. Mechanism-adjacent and consistent with the light-drinking.
Q-115 [ANSWERED — 29/B.11] Why can't Arthur just *sell* Use-10 recordings to the highest bidder? → He hasn't discovered Use 10 yet; when he does, the escrow exists precisely because selling to one faction paints a target for the rest (29/B.11: "possessing it makes him a target of *every* faction simultaneously").
Q-116 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why does the Garden write *once* ("The Garden never writes twice," 25/EVENT-095)? → Monastic signaling: one letter is a position; two is a negotiation (25). The novel must treat the single letter as the offer it is.
Q-117 [ANSWERED — 29/B.8] Why is "the universe does not choose holders" load-bearing? → Because chosen-one mechanics would license escalation; *presence* mechanics keep Arthur weak-by-design (29/B.8: "Arthur was not chosen. He was *present*").
Q-118 [ANSWERED — 29/B.6] Why is the ratchet one-way (no healing)? → "The lag is a clock, and it only runs one way" (29/B.6) — parallel to Erosion's irreversibility (02/I). The setting's clocks don't rewind; that's the theme.
Q-119 [ANSWERED — 29/B.11] Why doesn't Arthur just never deepen the lag (stay at 3s forever)? → The plot won't let him: the Kota Tua accident (Use 5) was involuntary; necessity drives deepening (Use 9: "the first time he needs to see a meeting he must not be seen at"). Refusal is a character choice with costs, not a veto on the story.
Q-120 [ANSWERED — 29/B.12] Why is "informed, not strong" enough for 500 chapters? → "In a world where information is the scarce resource (02/II), the best-informed weak man in Ravenscroft is the most dangerous kind of survivor" (29/B.12). Information advantages are renewable (sources shift); force advantages compound. The series engine is *staying* informed, not *being* strong.

## C. POWER, TIERS & ESCALATION (Q-121–Q-165)

Q-121 [ANSWERED — 30.1] Why don't T0s just shoot every Attuned (rifles beat channeling)? → "The tier that wins wars: 8 billion people, logistics, law" (30.1) — they *can*, and that's why Attuned register, hide, and obey: the gun is the real apex predator. The setting knows it.
Q-122 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why don't T4s rule cities? → 10–25 Fathoms per serious use; 3–8 year careers; "every use is a career decision" (30.1/30.2). Ruling is *sustained* output — the one thing tiers can't do.
Q-123 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why don't the three Worldtides just fix the High Tide? → All past 70 Fathoms, "dying by inches," one more major channeling would Drown Okonkwo (26.1). They're deterrents, not tools.
Q-124 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why does preparation beat tier (isn't that just plot armor)? → Seven mechanisms, each priced (30.3): rule interference, preparation, terrain, Tide-noise, information, Erosion attrition, social/legal constraints. The bible *prices* the underdog win instead of granting it.
Q-125 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why doesn't everyone just carry Tide-noise jammers? → Military-grade; 90 seconds of parity (30.3 #4); and jamming *is* a Seismograph-visible blind spot (RC-003) — carrying one announces you.
Q-126 [ANSWERED — 30.4] Why did the T5-vs-city scenario (Scenario 4) even need writing? → To prove the ceiling: "the city survives because it is *large* and *patient*; the T5 Drowns, usually before the army even arrives" (30.4). It's the anti-escalation thesis in one scene.
Q-127 [ANSWERED — 30.1] Why is tier "maximum sustainable output — nothing else"? → Because skill, preparation, and information aren't tiered (30.1) — the definition *creates* the weak-beats-strong design space. If tier meant danger, Arthur's story would be impossible.
Q-128 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why don't handlers just retire Attuned at 40 Fathoms? → Many do ("most retire by 55," 30.2); the ones who don't are the plot (debt, duty, Drowndust-era holdouts). The "past 45 Fathoms, everyone buys continuity" (04/08) is the economic reason some can't.
Q-129 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why doesn't Erosion attrition make every fight boring (just wait)? → "Don't win — last" *is* the strategy (30.3 #6), and waiting is dramatic when the waiting has costs (hostages, the Veil, the flare). Boring is a pacing problem, not a canon one.
Q-130 [ANSWERED — 30.1] Why can't a T6 Choir just *order* a city to forget (mass mind control)? → "No tier performs direct mind control" (30.1); suggestion seeding stays resistible, backlash-prone, unreliable at every tier (02/I). Crowd-steering isn't obedience.
Q-131 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why is there no T7 confirmed? → "The designation exists because it has been needed" (30.1) — 0 confirmed. It's a filing drawer for "we don't know what that was," not a boss tier.
Q-132 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why don't the strong just kill the weak's families (leverage)? → They do — that's why mechanism 7 (social/legal constraints) cuts both ways, and why the Tribunal exists (30.3). Leverage is priced into every faction's behavior; it's not free.
Q-133 [ANSWERED — 30.4] Why does Scenario 5 (Arthur vs T2 tracker) work — isn't "the Laggard eats his wake" convenient? → It's *accidental* mechanism 1 (rule interference): the tether drinks ambient information, so his wake is 3 seconds stale (29/B.2 + 30.3). The bible labels it accidental — camouflage, not a power.
Q-134 [ANSWERED — 30.4] Why doesn't the corporation just hire the T4 (Scenario 6)? → It does — "(c) offers his handler a better contract" (30.4). Institutions absorb individuals; that's the lesson.
Q-135 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why don't Attuned insure against Erosion (it's actuarial)? → They do — depth pay, Quiet-care, Erosion-care economics (36; 08). Insurance prices the risk; it can't *reverse* it (02/I).
Q-136 [ANSWERED — 30.1] Why can't Bone healers just heal Erosion? → "The healer's paradox: aggressive Bone healing *transfers* Erosion-risk to the healer" (30.1). Healing tissue ≠ healing pattern-wear.
Q-137 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why isn't "information advantage" just Arthur always winning? → Information decays: files update, the Veil edits others, factions learn he reads them and feed him (SERIALIZATION_AUDIT §4). The novel must show sources drying up — a discipline requirement, canon-supported.
Q-139 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why don't pre-Drowning Attuned (80–100 Fathoms) get used as suicide weapons? → They do — that's what Drowndust-era doctrine and Simjo-guk's program are *about* (EVENT-083; RC-005/009). It's not a gap; it's a war crime with a sealed Tribunal case.
Q-140 [ANSWERED — 30.1] Why can't Lanterns just read *everyone's* Tide-marks (surveillance state)? → Reading costs Erosion per use (30.2: deep Lantern reads are rationed); "every year together is measurably less of them" applies to surveillance too. Panopticons have Fathom budgets.
Q-141 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why don't wards just get raised in every fight (preparation is free)? → "Wards take hours to raise and seconds to matter" (30.3 #2) — and they're expensive architecture (04). Preparation favors the defender who *chose* the ground.
Q-142 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why is 100 Fathoms a hard number (not 99 or 101)? → It's a clinical scale, "maintained by the Compact's medical directorate" (04) — a measurement convention like blood pressure, not a physical constant. Drowning is pattern dissolution; 100 is where the scale *defines* it.
Q-143 [ANSWERED — 30.1] Why don't the Attuned just unionize against the Census? → Some do — the Night Clerks *are* a union in embryo (RC-040: "the Clerks unionize *against* the station"). The BPF's 2022 Census fight (EVENT-086) was broken by civil-service unions. It's happening; it's just slow.
Q-144 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why doesn't Tide-noise just get built into every building (passive defense)? → Cost, and noise *jams detection too* (36) — a permanently noisy building is blind to its own Seep events. The tradeoff is stated.
Q-145 [ANSWERED — 30.4] Why is Scenario 2 (clerk vs Choir interrogator) winnable — isn't "stubbornness" a joke counter? → Awareness *is* resistance per 02/I; the clerk's real weapons are triplicate notes (external memory) and the interrogator's license (mechanism 7). Stubbornness is the flavor; paperwork is the armor.
Q-146 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why don't T1s just… do crime (party tricks, no cost)? → Negligible Fathom cost (30.2) — and many probably do petty crime. The bible doesn't need every pickpocket; T1 crime is *beneath* the Seismograph and mostly beneath the plot.
Q-147 [ANSWERED — 30.1] Why can't Hollow slip into a warded vault if they *remember* it (recognition)? → "Hardened wards shred all non-whitelisted arrivals — recognized or not, the ward checks the whitelist, not the memory" (02/I). Memory isn't a key; the whitelist is.
Q-138 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why doesn't anyone just nuke a Place Seep? → Mundane force vs. Undertow pressure: the Seep isn't *matter* to destroy (03/30). You'd crater the city and keep the Rule. (Containment engineering exists because demolition doesn't work on pressure.)
Q-148 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why don't the Erosion-care costs bankrupt states? → ~$2B/year buried in defense/health line items worldwide (08) — "No legislature has ever voted on it as a line item." It's bankrupting them *quietly*; the High Tide trajectory is "the fastest-growing unacknowledged liability."
Q-149 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why is "the law is a leash the weak can pull" not naive (the strong ignore law)? → Because the strong have Census files, handlers, bank accounts (30.3 #7) — and unregistered T3+ channeling is globally visible (02/II). Law works on the strong *because* they're registered.
Q-150 [ANSWERED — 30.1] Why can't a T5 just *leave* (Slip away from the response)? → Intercity slip is T4-capable (30.1) but Slip costs Erosion "proportional to distance and unfamiliarity" (02/I) — a Drowning T5 can't afford the fare, and the Seismograph logs the departure anyway.
Q-151 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why don't the Worldtides' observations get *used* (they perceive the Tide)? → 26.1 Level 7: "perfect perception, zero transmission" — Okonkwo's debriefings are "famously courteous and famously empty"; Shirakawa's perception "doesn't translate well into language." The sensor has no output port.
Q-152 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why doesn't "rule interference" just get documented for every Resonance (a master file of counters)? → That's what the Ledger prices and the Red Ledger auctions (30.3 #5; 36): "Knowing the enemy's Rule… is worth more than two tiers." The master file *exists* — it's just expensive, incomplete (8–12% of vectors wrong, 26.1), and for sale.
Q-153 [ANSWERED — 30.1] Why can't Choir steer Arthur (he's not Attuned)? → Suggestion seeding works on minds, not patterns (02/I) — but Arthur's Use-3 anchor cross-checks *actions* against the shadow's record (29/B.11). He's *harder* to gaslight than most, not immune to influence.
Q-154 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why don't the militaries just draft all T3+? → 13 (militaries adapted): T3+ are "registered, watched, licensed — power with paperwork" (30.1); drafting them means owning their Erosion pensions and their Drowning liabilities. Conscription of the dying is bad economics.
Q-155 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why isn't Stillwater just in the water supply (dampen everything)? → "Side effects: emotional blunting" (04); Graywater is the street version (36). Mass-medicating cities is a war crime with extra steps — and it would blind the Seismograph's human layer.
Q-156 [ANSWERED — 30.1] Why can't a T2 Lantern just read the Laggard's Rule (it's right there)? → Kira (CHAR-033) "can *read* the Laggard's Tide-marks — she may be the first living person to *see* what Ilsa saw" (32/B) — the novel *plans* this. Lanterns read Tide-marks, not tethers; the tether's Rule is at the *other* end (29/B.5).
Q-157 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why don't the Drowned leave usable Seeps (Drowndust is banned but stockpiled)? → "Usually leaving a new Seep behind" (36) — *usually*, and the Seep is a hazard with an alien Rule, not ordnance. Drowndust is manufactured Erosion-weapon, not Drowned-residue (04/13).
Q-158 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why doesn't "endurance beats peak" make every Attuned fight last 90 seconds? → Because 90 seconds at full output costs a T4 ~15 Fathoms (30.4 Scenario 1) — the *threat* of the 91st second ends most fights before they start. Deterrence, not choreography.
Q-159 [ANSWERED — 30.1] Why can't T4 weather-working just… fix droughts (charity)? → "National-asset effects" with minutes-long sustainment and career-decision costs (30.1/30.2) — charity at 10–25 Fathoms a pop is sainthood with a pension plan. Some probably do; the bible doesn't forbid it.
Q-160 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why is there no "Erosion bank" (store unused Fathoms)? → Fathoms measure *wear*, not currency (04/36) — you can't bank the absence of erosion. Stillness *slows* further Erosion (02/I); that's the closest to saving, and it's just not-spending.
Q-161 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why don't the weak just always fight on thin places (home ground)? → Thin places are *dangerous* ground (pressure, attention — 29/B.11 Use 8: "it points at *pressure*, not safety"). Home ground favors the defender who *prepared* it (30.3 #3), not whoever stands on a thin place.
Q-162 [ANSWERED — 30.1] Why can't Bone just… regrow the Hollow Pattern (fix Drowning)? → Erosion is pattern-wear, irreversible at every tier (30.1: "No tier reverses Erosion — not even (especially not) on oneself"). Regrowing the pattern isn't healing; it's manufacturing a person.
Q-163 [ANSWERED — 30.2] Why don't the T6s' handlers just… let them Drown (they're suffering)? → They're "living deterrents" under "permanent Compact observation" (30.1; 26.1) — and a T6 Drowning *creates* a T6-class Seep. Letting them go is a containment decision, not euthanasia.
Q-164 [ANSWERED — 30.3] Why isn't the "seven mechanisms" list just eight (add "run away")? → Running away is *losing slowly* — and 30.3 #6 (Erosion attrition) already prices retreat as strategy ("don't win — last"). Fleeing is mechanism 6 with extra steps.
Q-165 [ANSWERED — 30.1] Why doesn't Arthur's T0 status make him *safe* (nobody bothers with T0s)? → 26.3: he's "the cheapest sensor in the system and the least protected" — T0 *plus* Loud *plus* tether *plus* claims access. Nobody's safe at that intersection; that's the premise.

## D. ECONOMY & FINANCE (Q-166–Q-210)

Q-166 [ANSWERED — 08/09] Why don't Gray Market millionaires just retire (they're rich)? → Wealth in the hidden economy is information-position, not cash (02/II); retirement means losing the network that *is* the wealth. The Tally (36) clears trust, not fortunes.
Q-167 [ANSWERED — 08] Why don't the five anti-billionaire mechanisms get named in my files? → They're in 08 §8.7 (per the final audit: "near-airtight… with the Pale Court as the worked exception"). Out of my read scope; the audit record confirms they exist and held.
Q-168 [ANSWERED — 09] Why do soundings have value if they're just ledger entries? → They're settlement denominators between institutions that *already* trust the Ledger (09/36) — value from membership, like correspondent banking. Not a currency; a clearing unit.
Q-169 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why doesn't Halcyon's precog-trading just win the market? → "Financial-precognition abilities exist at small scales" and every major exchange runs Lantern-screened compliance (02/II) — "lucky traders keep Drowning." The edge is real, small, and policed.
Q-170 [ANSWERED — RC-022] Why doesn't the Compact shut down Halcyon's thin-place data center? → It's a live conflict, not a gap: "the Compact's science directorate demands the training data" (RC-022 escalation). Shutdown is *one* of the contested outcomes.
Q-171 [ANSWERED — 08] Why doesn't Quiet Insurance just… collapse under High Tide volume? → Premium growth +40% since 2018 (26.2) lagging loss growth — it *is* straining; that's the plot (RC-026's taxonomy fights are insolvency wearing ontology). Collapse is a *trajectory*, not an event.
Q-172 [ANSWERED — 36] Why doesn't the Tally get robbed (it's hawala)? → Hawala clears on trust and reputation (36/09); robbing it means exile from the only clearing network. The enforcement is social, which is why it survives.
Q-173 [ANSWERED — 11] Why doesn't Vesper just buy NQA (vertical integration of test subjects)? → NQA is a mid-size Indonesian insurer with a branch war (RC-021), not a distressed asset; and Vesper's interest is *longitudinal subjects*, not the company (29/B.9). Buying the insurer buys the regulators' attention.
Q-174 [ANSWERED — 11] Why does NQA deliberately hire ~40 Loud staff (isn't that a masquerade risk)? → "High-attention hiring": Loud employees *remember* what Quiet claimants can't articulate (11) — it's "the highest-ROI line in the company's budget" (27). The risk is managed because Loud staff are still bound by NDAs and the Veil covers everyone *else*.
Q-175 [ANSWERED — 27] Why doesn't ML just replace Arthur (models flag 4,000/day)? → "ML proposes Rule hypotheses; humans confirm them" (27) — and Arthur "beats the sampling bound: he doesn't just confirm flags — he remembers the ones the model never flagged, and cross-references them months later" (27). The model can't remember what it never saw.
Q-176 [ANSWERED — 09] Why doesn't Argent Vault just get audited (it's a bank)? → It *is* the Gray Market's bank (CORP-008); RC-029's sounding run starts with "a rumor of a KNF audit." Audits happen — that's the conflict.
Q-177 [ANSWERED — RC-029] Why is a bank run "for silence, not cash" coherent? → Sounding-denominated accounts (09); withdrawing closes the paper trail (RC-029). The run is operational, not financial — the bible's distinction, and it works.
Q-178 [ANSWERED — 36] Why don't Kestrel Drops just get intercepted (physical couriers)? → Decentralized physical network (36/NET-002); CG-045 shows interception *happening* (a lost package). It's not immune; it's *resilient*.
Q-179 [ANSWERED — 08] Why doesn't depth pay just incentivize Attuned to burn out faster? → It prices the burn (36: "salary premium scaled to Fathoms") — which *reveals* the burn to the employer. Perverse incentives exist; that's why handlers and pensions exist (04/08).
Q-180 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why doesn't anyone sell "anomaly insurance" to the Quiet public (mass market)? → They do — that's Quiet Insurance (26.2 pillar 6): "post-exposure memory care and incident cover as a *consumer product*." Arthur sells it. The mass market *is* the product.
Q-181 [ANSWERED — RC-026] Why is the Place-vs-Event taxonomy worth billions (isn't it angels-on-pinhead)? → Because the phenomena exclusion turns on it (RC-026): "if it's a *place*, it's excluded; if it's an *event*, it's covered." Ontology with a balance sheet — the bible's best finance joke and it's load-bearing.
Q-182 [ANSWERED — 11] Why doesn't Meridian Re just… leave (it's a reinsurer, it can exit markets)? → It *is* the Quiet Insurance pillar (CORP-004); exiting the supernatural book means exiting its core business. The fight (RC-026) is cheaper than the exit.
Q-183 [ANSWERED — 36] Why doesn't "quiet leave" get abused (paid leave for exposure)? → "Paid post-exposure leave standard in 40+ countries, administered via Quiet Insurance" (36) — abuse is priced into the premiums, and claims need *exposure*, which needs a Seep. Faking exposure means faking a Seep report — which is fraud with a paper trail.
Q-184 [ANSWERED — 08] Why don't the corporations just disclose and go public (post-secrecy profits)? → Mutually assured disclosure (26.4): any discloser is implicated; and the deepfake dividend means disclosure *as content* doesn't move markets. There's no first-mover advantage in telling the truth.
Q-185 [ANSWERED — RC-048] Why don't the early remittances get arbitraged instantly (free money)? → "Someone arbitrages the time differential and the amounts start growing" (RC-048 escalation) — it *does*, and that's the crisis. The bible doesn't prevent the exploit; it prices the consequence.
Q-186 [ANSWERED — 09] Why don't the soundings inflate (someone issues too many)? → They're netting entries inside the Ledger's settlement system (09), not issued currency — inflation would require the member banks to accept unbalanced books. It's accounting, not minting.
Q-187 [ANSWERED — 11] Why is CORP-012 (Santara, "completely unaware") even in the bible? → 11: it's the control group proving the masquerade works — "a 41,000-person company can brush against Seeps repeatedly and learn nothing" — and "Arthur eats their noodles." Indifference needs a face.
Q-188 [ANSWERED — RC-024] Why don't the port's fictional manifests just get audited (customs exists)? → "Everyone controls just enough to be blamed" (RC-024) — the manifests are fiction *because* the inspection regime is captured. Auditing the fiction audits the captors.
Q-189 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why doesn't Tide-energy (Helios tapping pressure) just solve the energy crisis? → RC-028: "the energy is real and the grid needs it; the feedback is slow and deniable" — and the Helios Lattice *warned* about feedback. It's a live conflict, not a free lunch.
Q-190 [ANSWERED — RC-030] Why doesn't pressure-futures trading just… get banned? → "The pressure-futures buyers are identified and it's a *government*" (RC-030 escalation) — banning requires the banners to ban themselves. The bible knows.
Q-191 [ANSWERED — 36] Why don't Tide-brokers just retire on one big Rule sale? → The Ledger's auctions are bespoke (EVENT-084: a T4 *Rule* auction; Gray Market prices tripled after the Koi stole the docs). One sale makes you a target with a known wallet — retirement needs the *network*, not the cash (see Q-166).
Q-192 [ANSWERED — 08] Why doesn't the hidden economy just… show up in GDP (0.15% is tiny)? → 11: "the hidden economy is 0.15% of GDP" — it's tiny *because* it's 0.15%; the other 99.85% has quarterly targets. The smallness is the camouflage.
Q-193 [ANSWERED — RC-021] Why doesn't NQA HQ just fire the Ravenscroft branch (branch war)? → The branch holds the pre-1980 files (EVENT-096), the regional archive, and the growth engine (35: "Ravenscroft regional branch (growth engine)"). Firing the archive burns the asset.
Q-194 [ANSWERED — 11] Why does NQA's analytics team flag Arthur's recall pattern (isn't he just good at his job)? → "The analytics team's flags have since tripped on his recall pattern twice" (11) — because his recall is *exceptional even among the Loud* (16), i.e., statistically anomalous. Being too good at the job *is* the flag.
Q-195 [ANSWERED — 36] Why doesn't "depth pay" just get gamed (lie about Fathoms)? → Fathoms are clinically measured (04/36: "How deep are you?") — Lantern examiners read them. Lying about a medical measurement is fraud with a scanner.
Q-196 [ANSWERED — RC-047] Why doesn't the Gray Market just franchise the Market of Yesterday (it's profitable)? → "The vendors have a folk Rule for it (buy, don't ask, close by six)" (RC-047) — the Rule is *local* and folk-held. The Ledger "buys a stall" (escalation) and meets "professional arbitrage vs. folk Rule" — franchising a Rule you don't understand is how you get eaten by it.
Q-197 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why doesn't licensed conjuration just build everything (it's legal)? → "The economics *usually* favor mundane alternatives — conjured tooling is a premium product for sites where cranes can't go" (02/II). Legal ≠ cheap; Erosion is priced per job.
Q-198 [ANSWERED — 09] Why don't the member banks just leave the Ledger (it's a cartel)? → The Ledger is also the Tribunal's fine-settlement and the Compact's financial oversight (09/INTL-003) — leaving means leaving the *legal* hidden economy for the Gray one. Some do (that's the Tally).
Q-199 [ANSWERED — RC-013] Why doesn't anyone just… buy the Sulu Entrepôt's neutrality (the Ledger tries)? → "The Ledger tries to *buy* the port's neutrality" (RC-013 escalation) — it tries, and the port's operating system is the *dispute itself* (EVENT-073 "never actually ended"). Neutrality isn't for sale because conflict is the business model.
Q-200 [ANSWERED — 11] Why doesn't Naga Hitam's purchase of NQA's delinquent portfolio (EVENT-099) trigger regulators? → "A debt-portfolio acquisition; routine corporate paper" (25) — the public face *is* routine. The hidden truth (three of Arthur's reports inside) isn't on any regulator's screen.
Q-201 [ANSWERED — 36] Why doesn't "quiet leave" create a paper trail to the supernatural (it's administered)? → It does — that's Arthur's *job* (claims). The paper trail is the insurers' business; it's camouflaged as HR.
Q-202 [ANSWERED — 08] Why don't the Erosion-care billions show up in budgets? → Buried in defense/health line items; "No legislature has ever voted on it as a line item" (08). The invisibility is the mechanism.
Q-203 [ANSWERED — RC-025] Why doesn't Obsidian just cut the cable (Information Seep riding it)? → "The Compact orders the cable cut and three countries' internet notices" (RC-025 escalation) — cutting is the *escalation*, not the default, because infrastructure has users.
Q-204 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why doesn't the "information is the scarce resource" rule just make librarians the most powerful faction? → The Archivists (SUP-008) *are* disproportionately powerful for their size — they "certify the Compact's archives the way accountants certify books" (26.2). The bible noticed.
Q-205 [ANSWERED — 36] Why doesn't Graywater (diluted street Stillwater) just get legalized? → It's diverted/diluted (36) — legalization would require dosing standards for a drug with "emotional blunting" side effects (04). The street version exists *because* the clean version is controlled.
Q-206 [ANSWERED — 11] Why doesn't Pale Lantern just get exposed as a noise farm (it's industrial)? → Noise farming *is* its business (CORP-006, "industrializes noise farming," EVENT-067) — exposure would reveal *marketing*, not the supernatural. The farm's cover is that it's a content company. It is one.
Q-207 [ANSWERED — RC-028] Why doesn't Helios just stop tapping (the Lattice warned)? → "The energy is real and the grid needs it" (RC-028) — stopping means blackouts. The feedback is "slow and deniable," which is exactly how externalities survive.
Q-208 [ANSWERED — 09] Why don't the soundings get hacked (it's a ledger)? → The Ledger's settlement system with member banks (09) — hacking it is hacking *banks*, which is a different genre. The bible doesn't make its financial system magically hack-proof; it makes it *institutionally* defended.
Q-209 [ANSWERED — 02/II] Why doesn't anyone just sell the *map* of all thin places (it's data)? → The Cartographers' neutrality is "a professional fiction everyone exploits" (RC-037) — and the Double Map shows what happens when the maps leak: they're *already* for sale, via the Ledger. The market exists; it's just criminal.
Q-210 [ANSWERED — 11] Why doesn't the Branch War just get settled by org chart (HQ outranks branch)? → "Jakarta believes the file is sacred; Ravenscroft believes the file is *theirs*" (11) — and HQ ordered the pre-1980 transfer in 2022; "the transfer stalled" (11). Org charts don't move archives that don't want moving.

---

## E. SECRECY, MEDIA & THE VEIL (Q-211–Q-265)

Q-211 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why don't viral Seep videos just… go viral (the Veil edits memories, not phones)? → "Rot is proportional to pressure" (02/III): the videos that would convince are the ones that rot. Survivable footage is weak-Seep noise; the deepfake dividend (02/III) eats its credibility.
Q-212 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the deepfake dividend work *for* disclosure (AI could fake a *real* Seep convincingly)? → It does — that's the double edge: a convincing fake is indistinguishable from a hoax, so "video no longer counts as evidence *of anything*" (02/III). Real footage drowns in a sea of fakes.
Q-213 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why don't the Six Pillars just get listed in a leak (Snowden-style)? → The pillars are *practices*, not documents — "the masquerade isn't a lie; it's the absence of verifiable contradiction" (26.2). You can't leak the absence of a contradiction.
Q-214 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't the shelving protocol fail against screenshots (flood then bury — screenshots persist)? → The protocol's goal isn't erasure: "The protocol doesn't need people to forget. It needs them to *misremember* with confidence" (26.2). Screenshots of flooded noise are… noise.
Q-215 [ANSWERED — 26.4] Why don't the six anti-Snowden reasons just get *tested* (someone tries anyway)? → Lagos 2023 *was* the test (EVENT-089): 4,000 remembered for a week. "The experiment ran. Four thousand remembered for a week. Then the Veil did its work." The bible ran the experiment so the novel doesn't have to.
Q-216 [ANSWERED — 26.4] Why isn't the Lagos 2023 result (4,000 remembered) proof disclosure *works*? → "4,000 remembered for a week. Then the Veil did its work" (26.4) — the result proved the *failure mode*: memory without institutional support decays. The minority report's arithmetic argument stands.
Q-217 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the Veil cover the *Loud* (wouldn't evolution favor full coverage)? → Loudness is defined by the Veil barely touching them (36/29) — "birth or a Seep event — both rare" (33 item 7). The 3% are the exception that proves the mechanism.
Q-218 [ANSWERED — 26.1] Why don't the Quiet ever notice the Loud's 3% (that's a lot of people)? → 3% "remember something" (26.1) — remembering *something* isn't organizing. Loud recall is fragmented, individual, and deniable; that's why the Compact's L2 position is "a self-maintaining sensor network we don't have to pay for."
Q-219 [ANSWERED — 26.3] Why doesn't Arthur's extraordinary recall get him diagnosed (16)? → "NQA's occupational health *screened* him for it in 2022" (16) — it did. The screening concluded "exceptional autobiographical memory," filed it, and moved on. Being flagged as a good rememberer isn't a diagnosis.
Q-220 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why don't the Veil-proof (Loud) just write bestsellers (they remember)? → "Written accounts are the leak vector the professionals actually worry about" (17) — which is why the shelving protocol "prioritizes flooding *text* search" and why Quiet Insurance's media-monitoring exists (26.2). They try; the flood meets them.
Q-221 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't noise farming collapse under its own cost (it's industrial)? → Pale Lantern *industrialized* it (CORP-006) — it's a business with clients (brands, crisis PR). The cost is someone's marketing budget. Scale made it cheap.
Q-222 [ANSWERED — 26.1] Why don't Level 5 planners just *plan* the High Tide's reveal (they model futures)? → "The models… assume the masquerade holds *because they were built inside it*" (26.1) — the planning horizon's blind spot is named. They can't model what their models can't see.
Q-223 [ANSWERED — 26.4] Why doesn't mutually assured disclosure break (one madman leaks everything)? → Because "the audience forgets" (26.4 reason 2) — the madman's leak has no institutional memory to land in. Disclosure needs *receivers*, and the Veil unplugs them.
Q-224 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the "minority report" (26.4) win the argument (its arithmetic is right)? → Because the majority holds the *institutions* — and the minority report's own author notes "the minority doesn't need to win" (26.4): they just need the arithmetic to be *correct* when the High Tide tests it. It's a time bomb, not a policy.
Q-225 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why don't the insurance claims just… get denied (cheaper than paying)? → "A victim with an open claim is a victim who isn't posting about it" (26.2 pillar 6) — paying *is* the secrecy budget. Denial is the expensive option (lawyers, appeals, publicity).
Q-226 [ANSWERED — 26.1] Why don't the Level 1 Quiet ever get told (informed consent)? → They can't *retain* the telling (02/III) — informed consent requires memory. The cruelty is structural: you can't consent to what you can't remember being told.
Q-227 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why don't the Compact's own documents leak (it's a bureaucracy)? → Compartmentalization + the Veil: "every insider's knowledge is incomplete *by design*" (26.2) — a leaker can only leak their compartment, and the shelving protocol floods the rest.
Q-228 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the 60% T3+ coverage (26.2) leave 40% as constant exposure? → "Approximately 60% of T3+ events are covered within 72 hours" (26.2) — the other 40% are the *job*: the reason Sweepers exist, the reason Arthur's claims matter, the reason the High Tide is winning. The gap is the plot.
Q-229 [ANSWERED — 26.1] Why don't the Level 7 Worldtides just *write down* what they know? → "Perfect perception, zero transmission" (26.1) — Okonkwo's debriefings are "famously courteous and famously empty." Writing requires *translating*; the perception "doesn't translate well into language."
Q-230 [INTENTIONAL MYSTERY — 26.1/33] What do the Worldtides know that they aren't saying (26.1: "STATUS: UNKNOWN — author-level")? → Explicitly marked unknowable at author level and load-bearing: three human sensors with no output port. Resolving it would collapse the High Tide's dread into data.
Q-231 [ANSWERED — 26.3] Why doesn't the SMD just reclassify Arthur upward (they suspect something)? → The file was closed "for lack of a Hollow Pattern" (29/B.10) and the 2021 exam confirmed T1 (29/B.1). Reclassification needs evidence; suspicion isn't evidence — *that's* why the Cartographers' reclassification (EVENT-100) is the inciting incident, not the SMD's.
Q-232 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why don't the Sweepers just… quit (48,000, 12% burnout)? → "12% annual burnout" (26.2) — they *do* quit, at 12% a year. The system replaces them; the burnout is priced in.
Q-233 [ANSWERED — 26.4] Why don't the six reasons just get *numbered* as policy (make them doctrine)? → They *are* — "the doctrine taught to every new Compact analyst" (26.4). The minority report is the heresy file inside the doctrine.
Q-234 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the Veil edit *Arthur's* Laggard memories (it's supernatural)? → The Veil edits *Seep* memories in the Quiet (02/III); Arthur is Loud — "the Veil Reflex barely touches him" (01). The tether's own residue (shadow, dreams) isn't a Seep-memory; it's a *condition*.
Q-235 [ANSWERED — 26.1] Why don't the Level 4 operators just… tell Level 1 (whistleblowing)? → "Level 4… knows the *procedures* and suspects the procedures are a lid" (26.1) — telling Level 1 means the Veil un-tells them within days. Whistleblowing into a sieve.
Q-236 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't the claims-ML pipeline just *find* the Laggard (4,000 flags/day, Arthur among them)? → Arthur "beats the sampling bound" (27) — and the pipeline flags *Seeps*, not tethers; the Laggard's output is near-zero (29/B.1). The pipeline is looking for noise; Arthur is signal-shaped like silence.
Q-237 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why don't the dead zones (no signal) just get *mapped* publicly (they're geographic)? → They *are* mapped — by the Cartographers, who sell the Double Map through the Ledger (RC-037). Public mapping would publish thin places; the map is the contraband.
Q-238 [ANSWERED — 26.4] Why doesn't the "audience forgets" reason fail for *institutions* (they keep records)? → Institutions are made of people with the Veil (26.4 reason 2 applies to *readers*); and the records themselves are managed (shelving protocol, 26.2). The institution forgets because its staff forget and its archive is flooded.
Q-239 [ANSWERED — 26.1] Why don't the Level 6 principals just… retire (they're dying)? → "All past 70 Fathoms… dying by inches" (26.1) — retirement doesn't stop Erosion (02/I). They stay because the *position* (deterrent) outlives the person, and leaving creates a succession crisis (RC-002/042).
Q-240 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why don't the 330,000 events/year just… get *fewer* (containment working)? → "High Tide rising; T3+ share climbing" (26.2) — containment is *losing* arithmetically. The number grows because the pressure grows.
Q-241 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't anyone just *measure* the Veil (it's a cognitive response)? → Vesper tries — "longitudinal Loud studies," Nwosu's recruitment (29/B.9). Measuring it is the *research program*, not the refutation; the Veil is the phenomenon under study.
Q-242 [ANSWERED — 26.1] Why don't the Level 2 Quiet professionals just… publish (they know the procedures)? → "Knows the *procedures*… doesn't ask" (26.1) — publishing procedures reveals *process*, not the supernatural; and the procedures are classified. The leak would be boring *and* illegal.
Q-243 [ANSWERED — 26.4] Why doesn't the "evidence rots" reason fail for *witnesses* (people saw it)? → People are the Veil's substrate (02/III) — witnesses *are* the rot. The reason holds because the evidence *is* the witness.
Q-244 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why don't the media monitors just… miss things (they're human)? → They do — "approximately 60%" (26.2). The 40% is the miss rate, priced in.
Q-245 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the "cui bono" failure (reason 5) just get *explained* (who benefits from secrecy)? → "Everyone and no one" (26.4) — the benefit is *distributed* (every institution's continuity) and the cost is *concentrated* (the Loud's trauma). Distributed benefits don't need a beneficiary; they need an equilibrium.
Q-246 [ANSWERED — 26.1] Why don't the Level 3 Loud just… organize (3% is millions)? → "Fragmented, individual, deniable" (26.1) — organizing requires *shared* memory of *shared* events; the Veil ensures everyone's Seep is *theirs alone*. Loud support groups exist (IND-002) — as *support*, not as a movement.
Q-247 [ANSWERED — 26.4] Why doesn't the doctrine just *admit* the minority is right (it's 2024, the High Tide is here)? → "The minority doesn't need to win" (26.4) — admission would require *acting*, and the institutions can't act without breaking the equilibrium that employs them. The doctrine is downstream of the payroll.
Q-248 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the deepfake dividend just… get *fixed* (detection tech)? → "Detection is an arms race the detectors are losing" (02/III) — the dividend is *structural*, not technical. Better detectors make better fakes.
Q-249 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why don't the Six Pillars just… get *named* as the Six Pillars (it's cute)? → They're an *analyst's* taxonomy, not a charter (26.2: "the masquerade isn't a lie; it's the absence of verifiable contradiction"). Naming them would imply a designer; there isn't one.
Q-250 [ANSWERED — 26.1] Why don't the Level 5 planners' models just… get *better* (more data)? → "Built inside the masquerade" (26.1) — more data *inside* the blind spot doesn't widen the aperture. The models are precise about the wrong world.
Q-251 [PATCH REQUIRED → RED-008] The anti-Snowden doctrine says filmed evidence of a high-tier event "rots before broadcast" (29/B.11 Use 10) — but Use 10's *clean* recordings explicitly do NOT rot. Does the doctrine know about Use 10? → No one in-world knows Use 10 exists yet (discovery ~2024). But the *doctrine* (26.4) is written as universal ("evidence rots"), and Use 10 is a *known* future event to the author. The doctrine needs a "witnessed, multi-sensor, in-person" carve-out for non-rotting evidence — otherwise the first clean recording breaks a load-bearing wall of 26.4 without the bible noticing.
Q-252 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why don't the 48,000 Sweepers just… get *counted* (that's a census)? → They *are* counted — by the Compact's coordination, which is how the number is known (26.2). The count is internal; the census is the secret.
Q-253 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the Veil just… *fail* under High Tide (too much pressure)? → It might be — "the fastest-growing unacknowledged liability" (08), 60% coverage *falling* (26.2 trajectory). The failure is *in progress*; that's the series.
Q-254 [ANSWERED — 26.4] Why doesn't anyone just… *test* the Veil's limits (deliberate exposure studies)? → Vesper's longitudinal Loud studies *are* the test (29/B.9) — and the ethics are "impossible to leave" (29/B.9). The test exists; it's just not published.
Q-255 [ANSWERED — 26.1] Why don't the Level 4 handlers just… *quit* (they suspect the lid)? → "Suspects the procedures are a lid" (26.1) — suspicion isn't knowledge, and the pension is real (04/08: "past 45 Fathoms, everyone buys continuity"). The lid pays.
Q-256 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why don't the insurance premiums just… *price out* the Loud (they're the risk)? → The Loud are the *sensor network* (26.1 L2) — pricing them out blinds the system. The risk *is* the asset.
Q-257 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the "absence of verifiable contradiction" just… get *contradicted* (one solid proof)? → Lagos 2023 *was* the contradiction (26.4) — and the Veil un-contradicted it in a week. The absence is *maintained*, not natural.
Q-258 [ANSWERED — 26.1] Why don't the Level 6 principals' successions just… *happen* (people die, replace them)? → RC-002/042: "the succession is the crisis" — Worldtides are *irreplaceable* (26.1: "no confirmed T7… three living T6"). You can't succeed the irreplaceable; you can only *manage the gap*.
Q-259 [ANSWERED — 26.4] Why doesn't the doctrine's "six reasons" just… get *taught* to Arthur (he's the protagonist)? → He *lives* them before he learns them — the novel's pedagogy is experiential (29/B.11: "the order is the discovery order"). Teaching him the doctrine in chapter 1 would be a lecture, not a story.
Q-260 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the Veil cover *animals* (dogs react)? → The Veil is a *cognitive* immune response (02/III) — dogs don't have the cognition to edit; they just *react* (29/B.2). The bible's consistent: reaction ≠ memory.
Q-261 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why don't the media floods just… get *traced* (flood sources are visible)? → "Flood then bury" (26.2) — the flood *is* the trace; tracing it finds Pale Lantern's clients (brands, crisis PR), not the Compact. The cut-out is commercial.
Q-262 [ANSWERED — 26.1] Why don't the Level 3 Loud's support groups just… get *infiltrated* (they're a risk)? → IND-002 exists *because* they're managed — "Loud support groups" as an institution implies *oversight*. Infiltration is redundant when you run the venue.
Q-263 [ANSWERED — 26.4] Why doesn't the "mutually assured" part just… get *tested* by a dying principal (nothing to lose)? → The Worldtides *are* dying (26.1) — and their "debriefings are famously courteous and famously empty" (26.1). Even the dying can't transmit; the failure is *cognitive*, not motivational.
Q-264 [ANSWERED — 02/III] Why doesn't the 2018 attention-dip just… get *repeated* deliberately (tulpa farming)? → "The mechanism (which direction the causation runs) is UNKNOWN — 33 L5-4" — and Vesper's bet is STATUS: THEORY (33 L3). Deliberate repetition is *happening* (noise farming, EVENT-067) — the bible just doesn't promise it works.
Q-265 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why don't the Six Pillars just… *fail* one at a time (dramatic)? → They *are* — "the fastest-growing unacknowledged liability" (08), 60% coverage, Lagos 2023. The series *is* the pillars failing one at a time. The bible just doesn't number the acts.

## F. GOVERNMENT, LAW & SECRECY (Q-266–Q-300)

Q-266 [ANSWERED — 06] Why don't the governments just… tell their own citizens (democracy)? → 26.4's six reasons apply to governments *first* — and "mutually assured disclosure" (26.4): the first government to disclose implicates *itself* in the cover-up. Democracy can't vote on what it can't remember.
Q-267 [ANSWERED — 14] Why don't the courts just… admit supernatural evidence (it's real)? → 14: courts *do* — in sealed Tribunal-adjacent proceedings; the public courts get "depth-adjusted" procedure (CG-030's premise). The law didn't ignore the supernatural; it *sealed* it.
Q-268 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't the seizure doctrine just… get *abused* (states grabbing anomalies)? → It does — that's why the Tribunal exists (INTL-003) and why the doctrine has a "ladder" (26.2). Abuse is *priced* (referral, fines in soundings — 09).
Q-269 [ANSWERED — 06] Why don't the intelligence agencies just… *share* (they're all fighting the Tide)? → "Competence is distributed" (02/IV) — and *interests* are distributed too (12/34: faction relationships). Sharing helps the Tide; hoarding helps the *agency*. The agencies optimize for the agency.
Q-270 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't the Compact just… *enforce* (it has no army)? → 07: "no army" — the Compact *coordinates*; enforcement is member states' (26.2: "the Compact's closed verification protocols"). The weakness is the design: enforcement would require *jurisdiction*, which would require *disclosure*.
Q-271 [ANSWERED — 14] Why is "the weapon reverts" (borrowed-matter crime) not just… *legalized* (it's clever)? → 14: "the law can't quite grip the 72 hours in between" — the Compact *tried* to close it three times and failed. It's not legalized; it's *ungraspable*.
Q-272 [ANSWERED — 06] Why don't the states just… *draft* the Attuned (national emergency)? → See Q-154 — and 06's government file (per final audit) treats Attuned as *regulated persons*, not conscripts. Drafting the dying is bad economics (30.2).
Q-273 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't the Tribunal just… *publish* its rulings (transparency)? → Sealed by design (INTL-003) — publication would *be* disclosure. The Tribunal's authority *depends* on the seal.
Q-274 [ANSWERED — 06] Why don't the KNF and SMD just… *merge* (they're both regulators)? → Different states, different treaties (06) — and the KNF is "out of jurisdiction now" for Arthur (29/B.9). Merger is geopolitics, not org design.
Q-275 [PATCH REQUIRED → RED-011] 26.3 says Arthur "has never met another Loud adult" — but Saitō is Loud (34), Aisha is Loud (32/C), and 11 says NQA *deliberately hires* ~40 Loud staff. → Direct contradiction inside the relocation's key chapter. Either 26.3 means "never met another Loud adult *who told him what it meant*" (then say that) or the sentence is false.
Q-276 [ANSWERED — 06] Why don't the census files just… get *hacked* (they're databases)? → The Double Map (RC-037) shows what happens when the *maps* leak — via the Ledger, criminally. The census is the *target*; the bible's conflicts *are* the hacking.
Q-277 [ANSWERED — 14] Why don't the Echo rights just… get *settled* (it's been decades)? → CG-030 "The Echo's Pension" — it's *not* settled; it's a live conflict. The bible doesn't need it settled; it needs it *contested*.
Q-278 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't the "filed" status just… *protect* Arthur (he's filed T1)? → It does — "being filed T1 is the best ward Arthur owns" (30.4 Scenario 5) — *until* the Cartographers reclassify him (EVENT-100). The protection is *conditional*, and the condition just expired.
Q-279 [ANSWERED — 06] Why don't the governments just… *pay* the Loud (sensor network)? → 26.1 L2: "a self-maintaining sensor network we don't have to pay for" — paying would *create* the files, the payroll, the oversight. Free is the feature.
Q-280 [ANSWERED — 14] Why don't the Drowndust bans just… *work* (it's banned)? → "Banned but stockpiled" (my Q-157 formulation; 04/13) — bans *redistribute*, they don't eliminate. The Simjo-guk program (EVENT-083) is the proof.
Q-281 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't the 72-hour coverage target just… get *met* (60% isn't 100%)? → "Approximately 60%… within 72 hours" (26.2) — the target *isn't* 100%; it's the *funded* level. 100% would require the payroll the states won't approve.
Q-282 [ANSWERED — 06] Why don't the states just… *arrest* the Cartographers (they're criminals)? → "Neutrality is a professional fiction everyone exploits" (RC-037) — arresting the mapmakers blinds *everyone*, including the states. The criminals are infrastructure.
Q-283 [ANSWERED — 14] Why don't the wards just… get *mandated* (building codes)? → Cost (04: "built like temples") — mandating wards is mandating cathedrals. The code would bankrupt construction.
Q-284 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't the seizure ladder just… get *climbed* for Arthur (he's valuable)? → The ladder *is* being climbed — EVENT-100 (reclassification) is step one; the Cartographers' "experiment" (29/B.9) is the threat. The novel *is* the climb.
Q-285 [ANSWERED — 06] Why don't the intelligence treaties just… *cover* the Laggard (it's an asset)? → No treaty covers n=1 windows (29/B.12: "the classification system has no tier for windows"). The asset is *uncategorizable*; the treaties categorize.
