Q-286 [ANSWERED — 14] Why don't the courts just… *compensate* the Loud (they suffer)? → "Quiet leave" (36) and Quiet Insurance (26.2) *are* the compensation — medicalized, not litigated. The suffering is *insured*, not *adjudicated*.
Q-287 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't the Compact just… *count* the Loud (census)? → Counting requires *finding*; finding requires *testing*; testing 8 billion people for Veil-resistance is the census that *is* the disclosure. The count (3%, 26.1) is *estimated*, not enumerated.
Q-288 [ANSWERED — 06] Why don't the KNF just… *follow* Arthur to Albion (they're interested)? → "Out of jurisdiction now" (29/B.9) — and Aisha's unfiled follow-up (32/C) is the *personal* thread, not the institutional one. The institution stopped at the border; the person didn't.
Q-289 [ANSWERED — 14] Why don't the "phenomena exclusions" just… get *rewritten* (they're contested)? → RC-026: "taxonomy fights are insolvency wearing ontology" — rewriting the exclusion *is* the insolvency event. The exclusion holds because the alternative is bankruptcy.
Q-290 [PATCH REQUIRED → RED-012] Arthur's KNF/SMD file custody and Aisha's filing/non-filing chain conflict across 06/25/29/32 (see NAR-004). → The relocation's thinnest paper trail: who *holds* the file that protects him determines who can *move* it. The chain needs one canonical version.
Q-291 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't the "witnessed, multi-sensor, in-person" protocol just… get *used* on Arthur (verify the T1)? → It *was* — three examinations (29/B.1: 1992/2007/2021), all T1. The protocol *confirmed* the wrong answer; that's the horror.
Q-292 [ANSWERED — 06] Why don't the states just… *close* the Gray Markets (they're illegal)? → "Gray Markets" (36) persist because *enforcement* would require *jurisdiction* over zones designed to lack it — and because the states *use* them (the Ledger, 09). Closure is self-blinding.
Q-293 [ANSWERED — 14] Why don't the "three corpses in vaults" (Resonance theft, 02/I) just… get *investigated* (they're murders)? → They *were* — that's why the rule is *known* ("Three corpses in vaults prove why"). The investigation *is* the deterrent.
Q-294 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't the "biennial monitor" (29/B.1) just… *catch* the ratchet (it's measurable)? → See RED-009 — the 2023 exam's absence *is* the gap. If it happened, the ratchet (3.0→3.1s over 7 years, 29/B.6) is *within* measurement noise; that's why it didn't catch anything. The subtlety is the point — but the exam needs to *exist* on paper.
Q-295 [ANSWERED — 06] Why don't the governments just… *regulate* the Tide-brokers (they're visible)? → The Red Ledger *is* regulated — by the Ledger's own auctions (36/EVENT-084). Regulation *is* the market; the market *is* the regulation.
Q-296 [ANSWERED — 14] Why don't the "double-murder" statutes just… *deter* Resonance theft (it's double-murder)? → They do — "needs Tribunal warrant or it's double-murder" (02/I). The deterrence *is* the three corpses. The law works; it's just *posthumous*.
Q-297 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't the "seizure doctrine" just… *seize* the Laggard (it's an anomaly)? → "Filed" anomalies have *process* (26.2) — seizure requires the ladder; the ladder requires *classification*; the classification (T1 curiosity) doesn't authorize seizure. The paperwork protects him *until* the reclassification lands.
Q-298 [ANSWERED — 06] Why don't the states just… *share* the Seismograph (it's global)? → They *do* — "global Seismograph network" (02/II) — *coordinated* by the Compact, *operated* by members. Sharing the *data* isn't the problem; sharing the *interpretation* is (12/34).
Q-299 [ANSWERED — 14] Why don't the "quiet leave" laws just… get *abused* into disclosure (40+ countries)? → See Q-183 — abuse is *priced*, and faking exposure requires faking a Seep report (fraud with a paper trail). The law's camouflage *is* HR.
Q-300 [ANSWERED — 26.2] Why doesn't the "60% within 72 hours" just… get *published* (transparency)? → Publishing the *coverage rate* publishes the *gap* (40%) — which publishes the *events*. The metric *is* the secret.

## G. FACTIONS & RATIONAL ACTORS (Q-301–Q-355)

Q-301 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Garden just… *take* Arthur (they wrote once)? → "The Garden never writes twice" (25/EVENT-095) — the letter *is* the move; taking him would *break* the monastic signaling. The Garden's power *is* restraint.
Q-302 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't Vesper just… *grab* him (they're well-funded)? → "Impossible to leave" (29/B.9) — grabbing a *filed* T1 in SMD jurisdiction buys the seizure ladder (26.2) for an asset that *can't be extracted* (29/B.8: engineered transfers dissipate). The grab *destroys* the asset.
Q-303 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Choir just… *convert* him (street preacher tried)? → "A street preacher tried recruiting him in 2022 — failed" (29/B.9) — and Arthur's Use-3 anchor cross-checks *actions* (29/B.11). The Choir's tool (suggestion) meets his tool (the shadow's record).
Q-304 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Pale Court just… *seduce* him (Vivienne asks after him)? → EVENT-098: "Vivienne Ashworth asks after 'the Reed boy'" — the *courtship* is the thread; 33 L3 says Court eugenics is "predictively weak." Seduction is *in progress*; seizure is *useless*.
Q-305 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Red Ledger just… *auction* him (they auction Rules)? → "A buyer wants the Laggard" (EVENT-093) — the *Rule* is the lot; the *person* can't be transferred (29/B.8). The auction is *upcoming*, not overdue.
Q-306 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the KNF just… *reclaim* him (he's Indonesian)? → "Out of jurisdiction now" (29/B.9) — and the KNF's interest is *Aisha's* (personal), not institutional (32/C). The state stopped; the person didn't.
Q-307 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Alon Combine just… *hire* him (they want the analyst)? → RC-004: "The Alon Combine wants the analyst, not the analysis" — *recruitment*, not rendition. The offer is the *threat*; the novel hasn't reached it yet.
Q-308 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why don't the Cartographers just… *publish* the Double Map (it's valuable)? → RC-037: "neutrality is a professional fiction everyone exploits" — publishing *destroys* the neutrality premium. The map's value *is* its secrecy.
Q-309 [PATCH REQUIRED → RED-007] The Cartographers held Ilsa's journals for 28 years (1996–2024) without acting on the Laggard — why? → 29/B.3: "held by the Cartographers' Exchange"; EVENT-100 (2024-09-18) is the reclassification. 28 years of *inaction* on a filed window-anomaly needs a reason: did they *not read* them (then why hold them)? Did they read and *dismiss* (then why reclassify now)? The Exchange's inaction is the inciting incident's *precondition* — it needs one line.
Q-310 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why don't the Hollow Men just… *dive* to the tether's other end (they dive)? → SUP-006: they *do* dive; Crane's warning (EVENT-094) suggests "one already noticed it noticing." Diving isn't *understanding* — and 33 L5-2's resolution requires *Arthur's* test, not a diver's.
Q-311 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the SMD just… *promote* Arthur (he's useful)? → He's a *clerk* — promotion would *move* him from the claims desk (the asset) to management (not the asset). The system's *use* for him is positional (NARRATIVE_AUDIT §4).
Q-312 [ANSWERED — 12/34] Why don't the factions just… *ally* against the Tide (common enemy)? → 34 (faction relationships): the Tide isn't *an* enemy; it's *weather*. You don't ally against the rain; you fight over the *umbrellas*. The conflicts *are* the alliance politics.
Q-313 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Garden's letter just… get *answered* (it's an offer)? → "The Garden never writes twice" (25) — the *novel* must treat the single letter as the offer. Arthur hasn't answered *yet*; the thread is open.
Q-314 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't Vesper's Nwosu just… *ask* (she's hunting him)? → EVENT-091: "Nwosu's quiet recruitment inquiry" — she *is* asking; "quiet" is the method. The ask is *in progress*.
Q-315 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Choir's baptism just… *work* on the Loud (they're 3%)? → Suggestion is "resistible by awareness" (02/I) — the Loud are *defined* by awareness (they remember). The Choir's tool is weakest against its best targets.
Q-316 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Pale Court's eugenics just… *work* (they've tried for centuries)? → 33 L3: "predictively weak — inheritance raises odds, guarantees nothing." Centuries of *weak* signal — the Court knows; that's why they *court* instead of *seize*.
Q-317 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Red Ledger's buyer just… *name* themselves (EVENT-093)? → "A buyer wants the Laggard" (25) — unnamed *because* the reveal is a plot beat. The anonymity is *narrative*, not a gap.
Q-318 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the KNF's Aisha just… *file* the follow-up (she's an analyst)? → 32/C: "unfiled follow-up" — the *dog that hasn't barked*. Filing would *create* the paper trail; not filing *is* the character beat (personal vs. institutional).
Q-319 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Alon Combine's recruitment just… *succeed* (they're good at it)? → RC-004's escalation: "the Combine's offer *is* the threat." Success would *end* the thread; the novel needs the *offer*, not the acceptance.
Q-320 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why don't the Cartographers' experiments just… *start* (they reclassified him)? → EVENT-100 was *2024-09-18* — yesterday in canon time. The experiments *are* starting; the novel *is* the start.
Q-321 [ANSWERED — 12] Why don't the factions just… *kill* each other (they're rivals)? → "Competence is distributed" (02/IV) — and *costs* are distributed too (Erosion, Seismograph, Tribunal). Rivalry is *priced*; war is *bankruptcy*.
Q-322 [ANSWERED — 34] Why don't the faction relationships just… *stabilize* (it's been decades)? → 34: relationships are *dynamic* — the High Tide *destabilizes* (RC-002/042 successions). Stability was the *old* equilibrium; the series is the *new* one.
Q-323 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Garden just… *explain* the letter (it's cryptic)? → Monastic signaling (25/EVENT-095) — explanation would *be* negotiation. The cryptic *is* the message.
Q-324 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't Vesper just… *publish* the Loud studies (it's science)? → "Impossible to leave" (29/B.9) — publication would *reveal* the subjects. The science *is* the secret.
Q-325 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Choir just… *leave* Arthur alone (he's not interested)? → "They'd canonize the tether, not extract it" (29/B.9) — his *disinterest* is irrelevant; the tether's *theological* value isn't.
Q-326 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Pale Court just… *wait* (they're patient)? → They *are* — "Vivienne Ashworth asks after 'the Reed boy'" (EVENT-098) is *waiting*, done socially. Patience *is* the method.
Q-327 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Red Ledger just… *steal* the Rule (they're criminals)? → The Koi *did* — "Gray Market prices tripled after the Koi stole the docs" (EVENT-084). Stealing *happens*; it's just *priced*.
Q-328 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the KNF just… *forget* him (he's out of jurisdiction)? → Aisha *can't* — the unfiled follow-up (32/C) is *personal*. Institutions forget; people don't.
Q-329 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Alon Combine just… *outbid* Vesper (they're rich)? → They're bidding for *different* assets — the Combine wants the *analyst* (RC-004); Vesper wants the *subject* (29/B.9). Different auctions.
Q-330 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why don't the Cartographers just… *share* the journals (they're neutral)? → "Neutrality is a professional fiction" (RC-037) — sharing with *Pram* (EVENT-100) *is* the share; it's *targeted*, not public. Neutrality is *priced* access.
Q-331 [ANSWERED — 12] Why don't the small factions just… *merge* (economies of scale)? → 12/34: *niches* are the survival strategy — the Night Clerks (RC-040) *are* a merger in progress (unionization). Mergers *happen*; they're just *slow*.
Q-332 [ANSWERED — 34] Why don't the faction *enemies* just… *talk* (diplomacy)? → They *do* — the Ledger *is* diplomacy (09), the Tribunal *is* arbitration (INTL-003). Talking *is* the relationship; the conflicts are the *agenda*.
Q-333 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Garden's *silence* just… get *broken* (someone leaks)? → "Never writes twice" (25) — the silence *is* the institution. Leaking would *be* leaving.
Q-334 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't Vesper's *money* just… *win* (they're well-funded)? → "The most expensive bet" (33 L3) — money *buys* the bet, not the *outcome*. Vesper's *funding* is the *risk*.
Q-335 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Choir's *theology* just… *absorb* the Laggard (it's a miracle)? → "They'd canonize the tether, not extract it" (29/B.9) — absorption *is* the threat; it's *theological*, not physical. The novel must treat it as *doctrine*, not *theft*.
Q-336 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Pale Court's *marriage* just… *happen* (it's a romance option)? → 32's five configurations (CG-044 note) — the *choice* is the serialization decision (SER-004/RED-019). The marriage *is* the plot; it can't *just happen*.
Q-337 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Red Ledger's *auction* just… *close* (it's business)? → EVENT-093's buyer is *unnamed* — the auction *hasn't* closed; it's *upcoming*. The gavel is the *climax*, not the *premise*.
Q-338 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the KNF's *jurisdiction* just… *extend* (treaties)? → Treaties *are* the jurisdiction (06) — extending them is *geopolitics*. The border *is* the plot.
Q-339 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Alon Combine's *offer* just… get *refused* (Arthur says no)? → He *can* — RC-004's escalation is the *offer*, not the *acceptance*. Refusal *is* a beat; the novel just hasn't *reached* it.
Q-340 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why don't the Cartographers' *subjects* just… *escape* (they're scientists)? → "Scientific… the most dangerous kind: they will *experiment*" (29/B.9) — escape *is* the plot; the Exchange *is* the lab. The novel *is* the escape.
Q-341 [ANSWERED — 12] Why don't the *new* factions just… *appear* (it's 2024)? → They *do* — the Koi (EVENT-084), the Night Clerks (RC-040) *are* new. The bible *grows*; it just doesn't *announce*.
Q-342 [ANSWERED — 34] Why don't the *old* factions just… *die* (it's been a century)? → Some *did* — the Drowndust-era programs (EVENT-083) *are* the dead ones. The survivors *are* the adapted.
Q-343 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Garden just… *recruit* Arthur openly (it's an offer)? → The letter *is* the recruitment (25/EVENT-095) — *monastic* recruitment is *cryptic* by doctrine. Openness would *be* a different faction.
Q-344 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't Vesper just… *hire* the Cartographers (they're scientists)? → Different *sciences* — Vesper is *clinical* (longitudinal subjects); the Exchange is *cartographic* (maps, journals). The *methods* don't merge.
Q-345 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Choir just… *buy* the Laggard's *story* (it's media)? → Pale Lantern *is* the media arm (CORP-006) — the story *is* being bought; it's just *noise*, not *news*. The purchase *is* the flood.
Q-346 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Pale Court just… *outlive* the problem (they're immortal-ish)? → Erosion is *irreversible* (02/I) — the Court *isn't* immortal; it's *long-lived*. The problem *outlives* them.
Q-347 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Red Ledger just… *insure* the auction (it's risk)? → The Ledger *is* the insurer (09) — the auction's risk *is* priced. Insurance *is* the business.
Q-348 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the KNF's *analyst* just… *defect* (Aisha's personal)? → 32/C: the *unfiled* follow-up *is* the defection-in-progress. Defection isn't an *event*; it's a *paper trail* (or the *absence* of one).
Q-349 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Alon Combine just… *wait* (they're patient)? → RC-004: "wants the analyst, not the analysis" — waiting *is* the method; the *offer* is the *deadline*. Patience *is* the pressure.
Q-350 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why don't the Cartographers just… *destroy* the journals (they're dangerous)? → They're *archivists* (SUP-008 adjacent) — destruction *is* the heresy. The journals *are* the institution.
Q-351 [ANSWERED — 12] Why don't the factions' *rational* actors just… *be* rational (they're not)? → "Competence is distributed" (02/IV) — rationality *is* distributed; *interests* aren't *aligned*. Rational *for the faction* isn't rational *for the world*.
Q-352 [ANSWERED — 34] Why don't the *alliances* just… *hold* (they're treaties)? → 34: the High Tide *stresses* them (RC-002/042). Treaties hold *until* the pressure *exceeds* the *drafting*. The series *is* the exceeding.
Q-353 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Garden's *restraint* just… *fail* (someone pushes)? → Restraint *is* the power (25) — pushing *validates* it. The Garden *wins* by not *playing*.
Q-354 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't Vesper's *ethics* just… *stop* them (it's monstrous)? → "Impossible to leave" (29/B.9) — the ethics *are* the horror; the *funding* is the *license*. The monstrous *is* the method.
Q-355 [ANSWERED — 29/B.9] Why doesn't the Choir's *failure* (2022 preacher) just… *deter* them (it failed)? → One preacher *isn't* the Choir (29/B.9) — the *institution* doesn't learn from *street* failure. The *doctrine* is *patient*.

## H. MEDICINE, EROSION & THE BODY (Q-356–Q-405)

Q-356 [ANSWERED — 04/16] Why don't the Erosion clinics just… *cure* (it's medicine)? → "Erosion is irreversible. Nothing reverses it" (02/I) — the clinics *manage* (Quiet-care, 36), not *cure*. The medicine *is* palliative.
Q-357 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the Fathom scans just… *lie* (they're machines)? → "How deep are you?" (36) — the scans *measure* pattern-wear; lying would require *recalibrating* the Compact's medical directorate (04). The machine *is* the standard.
Q-358 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't Arthur's *exceptional* recall just… get *studied* (it's anomalous)? → 16: NQA *screened* him (2022) — "exceptional autobiographical memory," filed. Studying *further* would require *suspicion*; the filing *closed* the suspicion.
Q-359 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the "continuity" buyers (past 45 Fathoms, 04/08) just… *die* (it's expensive)? → They *are* dying — "buying continuity" *is* the dying, *priced*. The purchase *is* the palliative.
Q-360 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't the *Veil* just… get *medicated* (it's cognitive)? → Stillwater *is* the medication (04) — "emotional blunting" side effects. Medicating the *immune response* blinds the *patient*.
Q-361 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the Bone healers just… *heal* the healers (it's recursive)? → "The healer's paradox: aggressive Bone healing *transfers* Erosion-risk to the healer" (30.1) — recursion *compounds*, not *cancels*. The paradox *is* the limit.
Q-362 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't *exhaustion* (8s lag, 29/B.3) just… get *treated* (it's medical)? → It's *managed* (sleep, Stillwater-adjacent rest) — the lag *isn't* pathology; it's *physics*. Treating it would be treating *gravity*.
Q-363 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the Drowndust * antidotes* just… exist (it's a weapon)? → Drowndust *is* Erosion-weaponized (04/13) — the antidote would *reverse* Erosion (02/I: impossible). The weapon *is* the irreversibility.
Q-364 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't Arthur's *light-drinking* just… get *diagnosed* (it's visible)? → "Rooms dim slightly" (29/B.2) — *slightly*. Diagnosis requires *measurement*; the effect is *sub-clinical*. The 2020 Vesper scare (29/B.10 #5) *was* the workup — Fathom scan 0, file closed.
Q-365 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *hospitals* just… *ward* everything (it's safer)? → Cost (04: "built like temples") — warding *everything* is warding *nothing* (the budget *is* the triage). The wards *are* the ICU.
Q-366 [PATCH REQUIRED → RED-013] What are the escrow's *limits* — how much can the shadow hold, how long, and what happens at the boundary? (SER-001 overlap.) → 29/B.11 Use 7: "only what the shadow can cover; nothing living; retrieve within the lag window or it is gone." No *volume* limit, no *duration* limit beyond the lag window, no *failure mode* at the boundary. A protagonist who can stash *anything* shadow-sized *indefinitely* (within the window) is either invulnerable (stasis) or the limits need stating.
Q-367 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't the *occupational health* just… *retest* Arthur (the flags tripped twice)? → 11: "the analytics team's flags have since tripped on his recall pattern twice" — retesting *is* the threat; the novel *hasn't* reached the retest. The flags *are* the countdown.
Q-368 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *Erosion-care* billions just… *save* anyone (it's $2B/year)? → 08: "buried in defense/health line items" — the billions *manage*, not *save* (02/I). The money *is* the palliative.
Q-369 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't *Loudness* just… get *treated* (it's traumatic)? → IND-002: "Loud support groups" *are* the treatment — *support*, not *cure*. The trauma *is* the memory; treating it would *be* the Veil.
Q-370 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *Lantern* readers just… *burn out* slower (they're careful)? → "Every year together is measurably less of them" (30.2) — care *slows*, not *stops*. The burning *is* the reading.
Q-371 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't Arthur's *cold halo* just… get *noticed* (1–2°C)? → "On bad days" (29/B.7) — *intermittent* and *sub-clinical*. Noticing requires *continuous* thermal monitoring; nobody *thermally monitors* clerks.
Q-372 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *wards* just… *heal* (they're architecture)? → Wards *dampen* (02/I) — healing is *Bone's* domain (30.1), with the *paradox*. Architecture *isn't* medicine.
Q-373 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't the *Veil* just… *protect* the Loud (it's an immune response)? → The Loud are *defined* by the Veil *barely touching* them (36) — the immunity *is* the exposure. Protection *would be* the Veil *working*; the Loud are the *failure mode*.
Q-374 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *Fathom* scales just… get *recalibrated* (it's arbitrary)? → "Maintained by the Compact's medical directorate" (04) — recalibration *is* politics; the scale *is* the standard. The number *is* the institution.
Q-375 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't *sleep* just… *fix* the ratchet (it's rest)? → The ratchet is *permanent* (29/B.6) — sleep *manages* fatigue (29/B.3), not *history*. Rest *isn't* reversal.
Q-376 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *Drowned* just… get *autopsied* (it's science)? → "Usually leaving a new Seep behind" (36) — the *corpse* is a *hazard*, not a *cadaver*. Autopsy *is* containment.
Q-377 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't Arthur's *dread* (dreams, 29/B.7) just… get *medicated* (it's distressing)? → Stillwater *blunts* (04) — medicating the *dread* would blunt the *recall* (his asset). The dread *is* the data.
Q-378 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *clinics* just… *advertise* (it's a market)? → The market *is* hidden (08: "buried") — advertising *is* disclosure. The clinics *are* the secret.
Q-379 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't *exceptional* recall just… *fade* (it's 2024)? → "Exceptional autobiographical memory" (16) — it *doesn't* fade; that's *why* it's exceptional. The permanence *is* the anomaly.
Q-380 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *healers* just… *refuse* (it's suicide)? → The paradox *transfers* risk (30.1) — refusal *is* an option; the *duty* is the plot. The healers *are* the martyrs.
Q-381 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't the *light-drinking* just… *stop* (it's creepy)? → "Passive and always-on. There is no trigger, no command, no off switch" (29/B.4) — stopping *isn't* an option. The creepy *is* the condition.
Q-382 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *wards* just… *move* (they're expensive)? → "Built like temples" (04) — moving *is* rebuilding. The architecture *is* the asset.
Q-383 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't Arthur just… *quit* the night shift (it's killing him)? → The night shift *is* the claims desk (the asset) — quitting *is* losing the *position* (NARRATIVE_AUDIT §4). The shift *is* the story.
Q-384 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *Erosion* irreversibility just… get *challenged* (it's a rule)? → Vesper's bet *is* the challenge (33 L3: STATUS: THEORY) — "the most expensive bet." The challenge *is* the science.
Q-385 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't the *tether* just… show on *scans* (it's physical)? → "Fathom scan 0" (29/B.10 #5) — the tether *isn't* Membrane-physical (29/B.5). The scan *is* blind by *design*.
Q-386 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *Seeps* just… get *cured* (they're wounds)? → Seeps are *pressure*, not *wounds* (03/30) — curing *is* containment engineering. The wound *is* the world.
Q-387 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't *Saitō* just… *tell* Arthur (he's Loud, 34)? → 34: Saitō *is* Loud — but "has never met another Loud adult" (26.3; see RED-011) — the *telling* is the *gap*. The silence *is* the thread.
Q-388 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *hospitals* just… *hire* the Loud (they remember)? → 11: NQA *does* — "~40 Loud staff," "highest-ROI line" (27). The hiring *is* the policy.
Q-389 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't Arthur's *file* just… get *flagged* medically (it's anomalous)? → It *was* — 2022 screening (16), filed. The flag *is* the file.
Q-390 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *Drowndust* stockpiles just… get *destroyed* (they're banned)? → "Banned but stockpiled" (04/13) — destruction *is* disarmament; the *holders* won't. The stockpile *is* the deterrent.
Q-391 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't the *night* just… *end* (it's a shift)? → The *claims* don't sleep (26.2: 330,000 events/year) — the night *is* the volume. The shift *is* the system.
Q-392 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *Fathoms* just… get *hidden* (it's stigma)? → "How deep are you?" (36) — the *question* is the stigma; hiding *is* the lie. The depth *is* the identity.
Q-393 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't Arthur just… *sleep* more (he's exhausted)? → The *dreams* (29/B.7) — sleep *is* the dark water. Rest *is* the dread.
Q-394 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *wards* just… *fail* gracefully (they're old)? → "Built like temples" (04) — failure *is* catastrophic; the *maintenance* is the budget. The grace *is* the engineering.
Q-395 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't the *Veil* just… *lift* for Arthur (he's Loud)? → It's *already* lifted — "barely touches him" (01). The lifting *is* the condition.
Q-396 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *clinics* just… *merge* (economies of scale)? → The *secrecy* is the diseconomy (08: "buried") — merging *is* visibility. The fragmentation *is* the camouflage.
Q-397 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't Arthur's *recall* just… get *used* by the SMD (it's an asset)? → It *is* — he's the *cheapest sensor* (26.3). The use *is* the employment.
Q-398 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *healers'* paradox just… get *solved* (it's math)? → "Transfers Erosion-risk" (30.1) — the math *is* the conservation; solving *would be* reversal (02/I). The paradox *is* the physics.
Q-399 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't the *tether's* attention just… get *measured* (it's a signal)? → 33 L5-2: "whether the thing behind the Laggard is intelligent" is *UNKNOWN* (author-level) — measuring *would be* resolving. The unmeasured *is* the mystery.
Q-400 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *Erosion-care* patients just… *organize* (they're many)? → The *stigma* ("How deep are you?", 36) — organizing *is* outing. The silence *is* the shame.
Q-401 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't *Ilsa's* 3-hour cost (a week in bed, 29/B.6) just… get *documented* medically (it's data)? → It *is* — in the *journals* (29/B.3), held by the Exchange. The documentation *is* the contraband.
Q-402 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *wards'* costs just… *fall* (it's 2024)? → "Built like temples" (04) — the cost *is* the *craft*; industrialization *would be* the *profanation*. The price *is* the sacred.
Q-403 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't Arthur just… *tell* a doctor (it's medical)? → The 2020 scare *was* the telling (29/B.10 #5) — "Fathom scan 0, file closed." The telling *happened*; the medicine *couldn't hear*.
Q-404 [ANSWERED — 04] Why don't the *Drowned's* Seeps just… get *mapped* (they're new)? → They *are* — by the Cartographers (RC-037), *sold* via the Ledger. The map *is* the contraband.
Q-405 [ANSWERED — 16] Why doesn't the *body* just… *adapt* to the tether (it's 2024, he's had it since 2021)? → The *ratchet* (29/B.6) *is* the adaptation — *one-way*. The body *is* adapting; the adaptation *is* the clock.

## I. TECHNOLOGY, FORENSICS & SURVEILLANCE (Q-406–Q-435)

Q-406 [ANSWERED — 27] Why doesn't the claims-ML just… *replace* the Lanterns (it's 4,000/day)? → "ML proposes Rule hypotheses; humans confirm them" (27) — the models *can't* confirm; confirmation *costs* Erosion (30.2). The ML *is* the triage.
Q-407 [ANSWERED — 27] Why doesn't the Seismograph just… *pinpoint* (it's global)? → It *detects* (~T3+), not *pinpoints* — "unregistered Slipping" is *visible*, not *located* (02/II). The network *is* the alarm, not the address.
Q-408 [ANSWERED — 27] Why don't the *sensors* just… *see* the Laggard (it's 2024)? → "Output is near-zero" (29/B.1) — the sensors *see* channeling; the Laggard *isn't* channeling. The blindness *is* the design.
Q-409 [ANSWERED — 27] Why doesn't *Arthur* just… get *flagged* by the sampling bound (he beats it)? → "He beats the sampling bound" (27) — *beating* it *is* the flag (11: "tripped… twice"). The beating *is* the telling.
Q-410 [ANSWERED — 27] Why don't the *models* just… *learn* the Laggard (it's data)? → The Laggard's *data* is *absence* (near-zero output) — the models *learn* noise; Arthur *is* the signal-shaped silence. The learning *is* the missing.

## N. FORMAL ISSUES — RED-001..RED-021

> Every PATCH REQUIRED question above maps 1:1 to one issue below. Ten issues overlap NAR-###/SER-### findings from the companion audits (noted per issue; the parent may dedupe — they are re-issued here because this file's format requires it). Eleven are new.

### RED-001 — [MEDIUM] Laggard detection radius per use-tier is unspecified
- **Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.5 (counters), §B.11 Use 5.
- **Question:** Q-082.
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - "Deep use is also *visible* — Lantern-sensitives and certain instruments can detect it across kilometers" (§B.5).
  - "Lantern-sensitives *feel the wrongness*" [of Use 5 stepping] (§B.11).
  - No per-tier radius: is a 3-second Use-5 step "kilometers," meters, or only felt within 10m (cf. the §B.2 headache radius)?
- **Why it is a problem:** If every 3-second dodge pings kilometers, the SMD's instruments hold a log of Arthur's Kota Tua accident (EVENT-092) that the "thin file" (§B.1) never mentions — an undetonated contradiction. If the radius is small, the "visible across kilometers" line overstates the jeopardy the plot relies on.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) Add a per-tier detection table (Use 5: ~10m felt; deepening: kilometers; flare: regional). (b) Or state that Use-5 steps are *below* instrument threshold and only Lantern-proximate sensitives feel them — and reconcile with EVENT-092's aftermath.

### RED-002 — [MEDIUM] The smiling mirror reflection has no Rule, cost, or thread
- **Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.2 ("His mirror reflection sometimes smiles when he isn't — documented by Ilsa, observed by Arthur twice").
- **Question:** Q-086 (also Q-535).
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - The reflection is *documented* — twice observed, once in Ilsa's journals.
  - 02/IV: "if Arthur cannot eventually figure out *why* something happened, it should not have happened" (power-is-legible).
  - 33: the closed L5 set does not include it; no L4 thread registers it.
- **Why it is a problem:** A recurring, witnessed, *documented* phenomenon with no mechanism violates the bible's own legibility rule. It is currently either foreshadowing with no file to land in, or texture masquerading as mystery.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) Register it as an L4 thread with a planned payoff (e.g., lag-echo of his own future expression — still legible). (b) Or fold it into L5-2's territory explicitly (the thing behind the Laggard *practicing* expressions). (c) Or cut the second observation so it reads as single-witness texture, not pattern.

### RED-003 — [HIGH] The dead-man's-switch escrow has no mechanism
- **Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.11 Use 10.
- **Question:** Q-096 (also Q-569).
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - "copies… keyed to multiple faction and civilian recipients… released automatically if he fails to check in."
  - No holder, no trigger protocol, no anti-seizure design, no spoofing countermeasure is specified anywhere in 29, 26, or 09.
- **Why it is a problem:** The escrow is Arthur's *entire* defense against being disappeared — the load-bearing deterrent of the middle series. A deterrent whose mechanism is "automatically" is infrastructure doing heavy lifting off-page; any competent faction (Vesper, the Ledger, the SMD) would attack the *switch*, not Arthur, and the bible gives them no surface to attack — which reads as plot armor, not deterrence.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) Specify the mechanism (e.g., Ledger-escrowed sounding contracts + civilian dead-drops + a Choir-adjacent witness — each attackable, each priced). (b) Or downgrade the escrow's *claimed* reliability in-world (Arthur *believes* it works; the novel may prove him wrong — but then the belief must be characterized as belief). (c) At minimum, name who *built* it (Pram? the Exchange? Arthur himself?) — unbuilt infrastructure is the gap.

### RED-004 — [MEDIUM] Use-10 rot-immunity vs. deep-lag replays is untested at the edges
- **Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.11 Use 10; §B.6.
- **Question:** Q-100.
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - "The shadow is Membrane-darkness, not Undertow-structured information — so recordings of the shadow do not rot."
  - Deepening to 3 minutes / 3 hours renders *high-pressure, Undertow-adjacent* content in the replay (§B.6, §B.11 Use 9).
- **Why it is a problem:** The most abusable edge of the most dangerous use: can Arthur film a T5 event's replay *cleanly*? If yes, Use 10 + Use 9 breaks the masquerade's load-bearing wall (26.4's "evidence rots") with no counter priced. If no, the boundary condition ("recordings rot past X lag-depth") needs stating — it is currently the exact line every power-user would probe first.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) State the boundary: rot-immunity holds only for Membrane-darkness content; replays rendering Undertow-structured information rot *normally* (or proportionally to lag-depth). (b) Or price the exception (deep-lag clean recording costs permanent ratchet at a stated rate — making it a *choice*, not a *loophole*).

### RED-005 — [MEDIUM] Pram's destination: Jakarta (29) vs. Ravenscroft (25)
- **Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.3 vs. `25_TIMEmessaging app.md` EVENT-100.
- **Question:** Q-447. **Overlap:** NAR-001.
- **Conflicting statements (quoted):**
  - 29/B.3: Pram "was sent to Jakarta to deliver them [the journals] in person."
  - 25/EVENT-100: "Pram arrives in Ravenscroft with Ilsa's journals" (2024-09-18).
- **Why it is a problem:** The inciting incident's geography determines which branch (Jakarta HQ vs. Ravenscroft) holds Ilsa's journals — which determines the Branch War's leverage (11) and Arthur's access path. Both cannot be true.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) 29/B.3 → "was sent to Ravenscroft" (relocation-correct). (b) Or: sent to Jakarta, *redirected* to Ravenscroft — but then the redirect needs a cause (who redirected a Cartographers' courier?).

### RED-006 — [MEDIUM] GOV-015 / GOV-016 are listed stubs with no content
- **Location:** `35_CANON_DATABASE.md` (index entries GOV-015, GOV-016: "Content to be developed").
- **Question:** Q-441 (also Q-541).
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - 35 lists GOV-015 and GOV-016 as index entries whose "content is to be developed."
  - No corresponding files exist in the workdir listing.
- **Why it is a problem:** In a Albion-primary relocation, *government* entries are load-bearing (SMD jurisdiction, KNF vs. SMD custody, the seizure ladder). Stubs in the governance section read as unfinished relocation work — the exact thing a forensic audit is meant to catch.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) Write them (likely: SMD charter; Albion Compact-accession terms). (b) Or delist them from 35 with a note, if their content lives elsewhere (06).

### RED-007 — [MEDIUM] The Cartographers' 28-year inaction on Ilsa's journals is unexplained
- **Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.3; `25_TIMEmessaging app.md` EVENT-100.
- **Question:** Q-309.
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - 29/B.3: journals "held by the Cartographers' Exchange" (since Ilsa's death 1996).
  - 25/EVENT-100: reclassification *2024-09-18* — 28 years later, delivered by Pram.
  - 29/B.9: "Their interest is scientific, which makes it the most dangerous kind: they will *experiment*."
- **Why it is a problem:** A faction defined as *scientifically curious* sat on a documented window-anomaly for 28 years, then acted *yesterday*. The inciting incident's precondition (why *now*?) has no cause — did they not read them? Read and dismiss? Lose them in the Branch War's archive politics? Without one line, the timing reads as authorial convenience.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) One line: the journals were *misfiled* in the pre-1980 archive dispute (ties to 11/EVENT-096 — elegant). (b) Or: the Exchange *did* read them and classified the Laggard as "dormant — monitor" until the 2024 pressure readings changed the assessment (ties to the High Tide). (c) Or: Pram *found* them (he's a courier, not an archivist) — the reclassification is *his* doing, not the institution's.

### RED-008 — [MEDIUM] Use-10 vs. the anti-Snowden doctrine's universality
- **Location:** `26_INFORMATION_CONTROL.md` §26.4 vs. `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.11 Use 10.
- **Question:** Q-251.
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - 26.4 (doctrine): filmed evidence of high-tier events *rots before broadcast* — stated universally.
  - 29/B.11: Use-10 recordings "do not rot" (clean evidence is *possible*).
  - No one in-world knows Use 10 exists yet (discovery ~2024).
- **Why it is a problem:** The doctrine is written as a *universal* law of evidence; Use 10 is a *known-to-the-author* future counterexample. When the first clean recording appears, it breaks a load-bearing wall of 26.4 and the bible has no *doctrinal* room for it — the analysts' model of the world would be *wrong in a way the bible didn't plan for them to be wrong*.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) Soften 26.4's claim to "witnessed, multi-sensor, in-person *or otherwise non-rotting*" — leaving a doctrinal slot the analysts *know is empty* (dramatic irony: they're right about the shape of the exception before it arrives). (b) Or add the exception as an L2 answered question ("could clean evidence exist?" — "theory: yes; observed: never").

### RED-009 — [LOW] The 2023 biennial monitor: did it happen?
- **Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.1 ("monitor biennially"); §B.10.
- **Question:** Q-104 (also Q-294).
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - §B.1: filing requires biennial monitoring; examinations listed: 1992, 2007, 2021.
  - No 2023 examination is mentioned anywhere.
- **Why it is a problem:** The "protection expiring" timeline (EVENT-100 arrives 2024-09-18) needs the paper trail to be *complete*. A missing 2023 exam is either a *skipped* exam (why? austerity — RC-040? — which is plot-relevant) or an *unmentioned* exam (which should have yawned and confirmed T1 — also plot-relevant, because it deepens the irony).
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) One line in 29/B.1 or B.10: "2023: exam deferred (austerity backlog)" or "2023: exam completed, T1 confirmed, no change." (b) Either answer *feeds* the plot; the absence feeds nothing.

### RED-010 — [LOW] "Membrane" terminology note is a drafting Post-it in canon
- **Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` (local note, PROVISIONAL).
- **Question:** Q-108.
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - "Drafter A may rename it in assembly" — a *process instruction* embedded in the protagonist's canonical file.
- **Why it is a problem:** A v1.2 canon file should not contain assembly instructions. Either the term is canon (the note is noise) or it's provisional (the file ships a placeholder as canon). Forensic hygiene.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) Delete the note; "Membrane" is canon (it is used consistently across 29/33/36). (b) If the rename is genuinely pending, move the note to a drafting log outside the canon tree.

### RED-011 — [HIGH] 26.3 "never met another Loud adult" vs. Saitō, Aisha, and NQA's Loud hiring
- **Location:** `26_INFORMATION_CONTROL.md` §26.3 vs. `34_FACTION_RELATIONSHIPS.md` (Saitō), `32_ROMANCE_FRAMEWORK.md` §C (Aisha), `11_CORPORATIONS.md` (NQA hiring).
- **Question:** Q-275. **Overlap:** NAR-003.
- **Conflicting statements (quoted):**
  - 26.3: Arthur "has never met another Loud adult."
  - 34: Saitō *is* Loud. 32/C: Aisha *is* Loud. 11: NQA *deliberately hires* ~40 Loud staff — Arthur works there.
- **Why it is a problem:** Direct factual contradiction in the relocation's key chapter. Arthur's isolation ("never met another Loud adult") is *thematically* load-bearing (his loneliness, his vulnerability to the first Loud who *does* tell him) — but it is *factually* false on the bible's own terms unless "met" means something narrower.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) 26.3 → "has never met another Loud adult *who told him what it meant*" (preserves the theme, fixes the fact). (b) Or: Saitō/Aisha's Loudness is *unknown to Arthur* (then 26.3 needs "knowingly" — and the dramatic irony becomes explicit, which is *better*).

### RED-012 — [HIGH] Arthur's KNF/SMD file custody chain is inconsistent
- **Location:** `06_GOVERNMENTS.md` / `25_TIMEmessaging app.md` / `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` / `32_ROMANCE_FRAMEWORK.md` §C.
- **Question:** Q-290. **Overlap:** NAR-004.
- **Conflicting statements (quoted):** (per NAR-004's collation) the custody of Arthur's file — who *holds* it, who *filed* what, and whether Aisha's follow-up was *filed or not* — differs across the four files.
- **Why it is a problem:** The file *is* Arthur's legal existence (visa, T1 filing, seizure-ladder protection). Who *holds* it determines who can *move* him. An inconsistent custody chain means the novel's central legal jeopardy has no canonical answer to the question "who do they call?"
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) Publish one custody table (KNF opened → SMD inherited on relocation → Aisha's follow-up *unfiled* per 32/C). (b) Reconcile all four files to it.

### RED-013 — [MEDIUM] Use-7 escrow limits are unstated (stasis/invulnerability risk)
- **Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.11 Use 7.
- **Question:** Q-366. **Overlap:** SER-001.
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - "Only what the shadow can cover; nothing living; retrieve within the lag window or it is gone."
  - No *volume* limit, no *duration* beyond the lag window, no *failure mode* at the boundary.
- **Why it is a problem:** Serialization risk: a protagonist who can stash *anything shadow-sized* with *total-loss* as the only downside trends toward either invulnerability (stash the evidence, the weapon, the hostage) or stasis (never use it because the risk is total). The middle — *priced, limited, interesting* use — needs numbers.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) State a volume cap (e.g., "what one hand can cover") and a *retrieval cost* (each retrieval deepens the lag fractionally — tie to the ratchet). (b) Or state the boundary failure *gradient* (not binary total-loss: the longer past the window, the *less* returns — "the shadow keeps a tithe").

### RED-014 — [LOW] CG-044 mislabeled as "the KNF mole hunt" in 32
- **Location:** `32_ROMANCE_FRAMEWORK.md` (CG-044 note) vs. `35_CANON_DATABASE.md` (RC index).
- **Question:** Q-506. **Overlap:** NAR-002.
- **Conflicting statements (quoted):**
  - 32/CG-044 note: "the KNF mole hunt."
  - 35/RC index: CG-044 is the *BPF* mole hunt; the KNF mole is RC-032.
- **Why it is a problem:** Cross-reference error in the romance framework's load-bearing conflict note. A reader (or the novel) following the note chases the *wrong* mole in the *wrong* agency.
- **Proposed solutions:** 32's note → "the BPF mole hunt (CG-044)" or "the KNF mole (RC-032)" — whichever the scene needs.

### RED-015 — [MEDIUM] Use-12's flare has no priced permanent consequence
- **Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.11 Use 12.
- **Question:** Q-526. **Overlap:** SER-005.
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - "A flare he can't unfire… everyone comes. Everyone" — "a point of no return."
  - No *permanent* consequence for the T1 filing is specified (reclassification? seizure? burning the escrow?).
- **Why it is a problem:** "Point of no return" with no *priced* return is a *threat* without a *bill*. The novel needs to know what the flare *costs* — not just who *comes* — or the climax's stakes are atmospheric rather than mechanical.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) Price it: the flare *burns* the T1 filing permanently (reclassification to *unfiled window* — the seizure ladder's top rung). (b) Or: the flare *marks* him Seismograph-visible *forever* (the "kilometers" of RED-001 becomes *permanent*). (c) Either way, the *first* flare must *close* the "filed T1" era — that closure is the *cost*.

### RED-016 — [MEDIUM] The 12-use ladder has no ratchet ruler
- **Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.6; §B.11.
- **Question:** Q-531. **Overlap:** SER-003.
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - §B.6: "3.0→3.1s over ~7 years" (baseline drift); extreme uses add *permanent* fractions.
  - No per-use *amount* is stated for any of the 12 documented uses.
- **Why it is a problem:** The ratchet is the series' *clock* (29/B.6: "it only runs one way"). A clock with no *numbers* can't be *planned* — the novel can't pace the approach to 10s (L5-5's terminus) without a ruler. Serialization needs the *budget*.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) A ratchet table: each documented use's *permanent* addition (e.g., Use 5: +0.01s; Use 9: +0.1s; Use 12: +1.0s — illustrative, not prescriptive). (b) Or a *principle*: permanent addition scales with *lag-depth × Undertow-pressure* (a formula, not a table — preserves flexibility).

### RED-017 — [LOW] 35's index contains stale pre-relocation material
- **Location:** `35_CANON_DATABASE.md` (RC summaries; coordinator note).
- **Question:** Q-532. **Overlap:** NAR-005.
- **Conflicting statements (quoted):**
  - 35/coordinator note: "Arthur retargeted … Liwanag City" (pre-relocation geography).
- **Why it is a problem:** The index is the *map*; stale entries misdirect every downstream user (including this audit's Enemy simulation). Forensic hygiene for a v1.2 relocation.
- **Proposed solutions:** Sweep 35 for pre-relocation residue (Liwanag City, Jakarta-primary framings) and reconcile to Ravenscroft-primary.

### RED-018 — [HIGH] No canon principle authorizes Uses 13+ (post-ladder novelty)
- **Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.11 (the ladder ends at 12).
- **Question:** Q-521. **Overlap:** SER-002.
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - §B.11 documents *exactly* 12 uses in *discovery order* — "the novel's progression spine."
  - The series needs 400–600 chapters (SERIALIZATION_AUDIT); the ladder is *climbable* within the first 100–200.
- **Why it is a problem:** After the 12th discovery, the protagonist's *novelty engine* (new observations → new uses) has no *licensed* source. The novel must either *repeat* uses (stasis), *invent* uses off-ladder (unpriced escalation), or *deepen* existing uses (which needs RED-016's ruler). The bible *budgets* 12 discoveries for a 500-chapter series — the arithmetic doesn't close.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) A *generative principle*: new uses emerge from *new lag-depths* (the ratchet *itself* unlocks observations — the clock *is* the ladder's extension). (b) Or: the journals contain *Ilsa's* unattempted uses (the *Exchange* holds the *syllabus*). (c) Or: cap the series' *first* 500 chapters at the 12-use spine (the *deepening*, not the *widening*, is the engine — but then say so).

### RED-019 — [MEDIUM] Five romance configurations "planned," none chosen
- **Location:** `32_ROMANCE_FRAMEWORK.md` (CG-044 note: five configurations).
- **Question:** Q-507 (also Q-519). **Overlap:** SER-004.
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - 32 lists *five* romance configurations as "planned."
  - No *decision* is recorded; the *choice* determines Aisha's arc, the KNF thread, and the Pale Court courtship.
- **Why it is a problem:** Not a *canon* bug — a *serialization* decision the canon *defers*. But the deferral *is* load-bearing: the novel's *first* 50 chapters need to know which *threads* to plant. "Planned" isn't a *plan*.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) Choose one as *primary* and demote four to *alternates* (the audit's recommendation: decide before chapter 1). (b) Or: canonize the *indecision* as a *love-pentagon* — but then the *jealousy mechanics* need pricing (see 32's framework).

### RED-020 — [MEDIUM] The escrow's *reliability* is asserted, not characterized
- **Location:** `29_PROTAGONIST_ANOMALY.md` §B.11 Use 10 (escrow paragraph).
- **Question:** Q-535 (paired with RED-003).
- **Conflicting / missing statements (quoted):**
  - The escrow is presented as *functional* deterrence ("released automatically").
  - No *failure mode*, *test*, or *in-world doubt* is recorded.
- **Why it is a problem:** (Companion to RED-003.) Even *with* a mechanism, a deterrent the *protagonist* trusts *completely* and the *reader* never sees *tested* is *tell-don't-show* protection. The novel needs the escrow *doubted* (by Arthur, by a faction, by a near-miss) before it's *relied upon*.
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) Add an *in-world* doubt beat (Pram: "you *tested* this?" — Arthur: silence). (b) Or a *near-miss* in the timeline (a faction *probes* the switch and *almost* trips it). (c) Fold into RED-003's fix: the mechanism's *specification* should include its *known* weaknesses.

### RED-021 — [LOW] The Fukushima Rule's content drifts (treaty vs. doctrine)
- **Location:** `24_HISTORY.md` / `25_TIMEmessaging app.md` vs. `31_CONFLICT_ENGINE.md` RC-008.
- **Question:** Q-538 (also Q-417).
- **Conflicting statements (quoted):**
  - 24/25: the Fukushima Rule — "some Tide-marks too sacred to exploit" (read-only by treaty).
  - 31/RC-008: the Fukushima Rule — "some places can't be contained, only managed around" (containment doctrine).
- **Why it is a problem:** *Same name, different rule.* A treaty about *Tide-marks* and a doctrine about *maritime containment* are not the same instrument. The drift suggests the relocation (or an earlier draft) *reused* the name for a new purpose — which *undermines* the history's *weight* (Fukushima is *sensitive*; the naming *is* the canon — Q-539).
- **Proposed solutions:** (a) The Rule has *two clauses* (treaty + doctrine) — then *say* it has two clauses, in 24. (b) Or RC-008 *mislabels* the manage-around doctrine — then *rename* RC-008's doctrine (e.g., "the Ravenscroft Doctrine" — relocation-appropriate). (c) Either way, *one* name, *one* meaning.

2. **The 2020 attention-pressure datum (EVENT-081)** — the *causation direction* is L5-4 (INTENTIONAL); the *datum itself* (global attention dip → Seep pressure dip) is *stated* but its *measurement* (who measured? how?) is not in the read files. If the datum is *unmeasured*, Vesper's bet (33 L3) rests on *anecdote*. **HOLD for 27/33 full-read.**
3. **The Pale Court's "worked exception"** — the final audit claims the Court is the *worked* exception to the anti-billionaire mechanisms. The *mechanics* of the exception are not in the read files. A hostile reader should verify the *exception* doesn't *swallow* the rule. **HOLD for 08/11 full-read.**
4. **GOV-015/016's intended content** — RED-006 flags the stubs; the *intent* (SMD charter? Albion accession terms?) is *unrecoverable* from the read files. The *parent* (relocation authority) may know. **HOLD for parent.**
5. **The "three corpses in vaults" (02/I)** — cited as the Resonance-theft deterrent; the *cases* are not in the read files. If the cases are *unwritten*, the deterrent is *asserted*, not *shown*. **HOLD for 04/14 full-read.**
6. **RC-040's unionization mechanics** — "the Clerks unionize *against* the station" is *stated*; the *how* (which union? what leverage?) is not in the read files. Labor law vs. secrecy is a *rich* attack surface. **HOLD for 18 full-read.**
7. **The 2023 monitor (RED-009)** — unresolvable without the *parent's* intent (deferred? completed?). **HOLD for parent.**
8. **Uses 13+ (RED-018)** — the *generative principle* is a *design* decision, not an *audit* finding. The audit *flags* the gap; the *parent/author* must *choose*. **HOLD for parent/author.**
